Abstract: The Prague Dependency Treebank framework is unique in its attempt to systematically include and link different layers of language, including a meaning representation with several types of inter-sentential phenomena, especially coreference and discourse relations. We present its second consolidated version (PDT-C 2.0), which concludes almost 30-years long project of sustained development of the resource to a uniformly and coherently annotated, genre-diversified, almost 4 million token language resource of Czech language, with accompanying fully compatible lexicons. In addition to continuous linguistic research, the richly linguistically annotated corpus is also widely used in international comparisons of the development of traditional and novel NLP tools as well as in conversions into other formalisms. The corpus and the trained parsers are available under the CC BY-NC-SA licence.

</details>


### [36] [Transformer-Based Language Models Across Domain Verticals: Architectures, Applications and Critical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24331)
*Guruprakash J,Krithika L. B*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对基于Transformer的语言模型进行了系统综述，从机制和使用两个层面，分类梳理了主要Transformer变体（编码器-only、解码器-only、编码器-解码器、长上下文、置换式、生成器-判别器）及2023年后关键发展（指令微调、RLHF、DPO、MoE、检索增强等），并评估了各架构在部署决策中的四维表现，讨论了参数量与能耗的权衡以及对齐方法、数据来源和基准饱和对“最先进”含义的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based语言模型已成为NLP的默认基础技术，但新模型发布节奏过快，从业者难以区分核心创新与增量式改进，亟需一份系统性的综述来梳理技术脉络和实际部署价值。

Method: 采用双层综述方法：在机制层面，对Transformer家族进行系统分类（编码器-only、解码器-only、编码器-解码器、长上下文、置换式、生成器-判别器），并延伸至2023年后实践中的重要发展（指令微调、RLHF、DPO、MoE、检索增强等）；在使用层面，调研医疗、金融、法律、教育、客服、创意写作和科学工作等领域的部署情况，将具体能力与Transformer的适用性关联。

Result: 构建了Transformer主要变体的工作分类体系；在影响部署决策的四个维度上进行了架构对比；量化了参数量与能耗之间的权衡关系；揭示了对齐方法、数据来源和基准饱和如何改变“最先进”模型的评判标准。

Conclusion: 本文提供了基于系统调研的批判性评估，指出了当前“最先进”概念因对齐方法、数据来源和基准饱和而变得模糊，并列出值得未来更多关注的研究问题。

Abstract: Transformer-based language models have become the default substrate for natural language processing and the pace of new releases has made it hard for practitioners to separate durable ideas from the noise of incremental announcements. This review works at two levels. At the level of mechanism, we organise the main transformer families into a working taxonomy, covering encoder-only, decoder-only, encoder-decoder, long-context, permutation-based, and generator-discriminator variants. We then extend the discussion to post-2023 developments that changed the picture in practice: instruction tuning, reinforcement learning from human feedback, direct preference optimisation, mixture-of-experts scaling, retrieval augmentation and the current flagship model families from OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, Meta, Mistral and DeepSeek. At the level of use, we survey deployments across healthcare, finance, legal, education, customer service, creative writing and scientific work. Based on this we link each to the specific capabilities that make a transformer the appropriate tool. The contribution of this paper is a critical assessment that is based on the survey. We compare architectures on four axes that matter to deployment decisions, we quantify the trade-off between parameter count and energy cost. We also discuss how alignment methods, data provenance and benchmark saturation change what it means to call a model "state of the art". The final section lists the research questions that we think deserve more attention.

</details>


### [37] [Meet UD_Czech-PDTC: A Large and Genre-Rich Treebank in Universal Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24337)
*Marie Mikulová,Barbora Štěpánková,Daniel Zeman,Jan Štěpánek,Milan Straka,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了将捷克语 Prague Dependency Treebank-Consolidated (PDT-C) 转换为 Universal Dependencies (UD) 的过程。PDT-C 比原始 PDT 规模大两倍以上，涵盖更多体裁和领域。尽管两种标注方案表面相似，但在依存结构拓扑、POS和关系类型粒度上存在诸多差异。文章通过示例展示这些差异，讨论转换方法，并论证 PDT 的多层标注虽不"通用"但信息丰富，足以生成完整的 UD 树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将新构建的更大规模、更多样化的捷克语 PDT-C 资源转换为 Universal Dependencies 格式，使该资源能够被更广泛的跨语言研究社区使用，并揭示两种标注方案之间的深层差异。

Method: 对比分析 Prague Dependency Treebank 与 Universal Dependencies 两种标注方案在依存结构拓扑、词性标注和关系类型粒度上的差异，通过具体示例展示不同之处，并设计相应的转换策略来克服这些差异。

Result: 成功将 PDT-C 转换为 UD 格式，识别并处理了两种标注方案之间大量细微的拓扑结构和粒度差异，同时保留了原始资源丰富的多层标注信息。

Conclusion: PDT 虽然不如 UD "通用"且更紧密绑定于单一语言，但其多层标注信息丰富，不仅提供构建基本 UD 树所需的所有信息，还包含更多额外内容。

Abstract: Czech has been part of Universal Dependencies since its first release in 2015. It has also been one of the best represented languages, with the Prague Dependency Treebank being order of magnitude larger than most other UD treebanks. More recently, three other datasets from the Prague family were added and the annotations thoroughly revisited, forming the "Prague Dependency Treebank-Consolidated" (PDT-C). In comparison to the original PDT, PDT-C is more than twice as large, but it is also much more diverse in terms of genres and domains. In this paper, we describe the conversion of the new resource to Universal Dependencies. While the two annotation schemes are relatively similar at the first sight, there are numerous small differences in topology of the dependency structures and in granularity of the POS and relation type inventories. We demonstrate a selection of such differences on examples, discuss the diverging motivations, as well as ways to overcome the differences during conversion. We argue that while PDT is less "universal" and more tightly bound to one language, its multi-layer annotation is rich and provides all information needed for basic UD trees, and much more.

</details>


### [38] [Automatic Part-of-Speech Tagging of Arabic-English Dictionary Senses through WordNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24359)
*Diaa M. Fayed,Aly A. Fahmy,Mohsen A. Rashwan,Wafaa K. Fayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用英语翻译等价物的POS标签对双语词典词条进行词性标注的算法，应用于阿拉伯语-英语词典，通过消除歧义后将英语WordNet的POS标签转移到词典义项上，准确率高且成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源匮乏语言的NLP工具开发需要大量语言学专家、资金和时间，统计方法需要大规模标注语料库，规则方法需要丰富的词典知识。因此需要轻资源方法，而双语词典词条的POS标注是连接双语词典与WordNet或标准化为WordNet-LMF格式的前提条件。

Method: 将英语翻译等价物的POS标签经过消歧过程后转移到双语词典的义项上，英语义项的POS标签从Princeton WordNet获取。

Result: 该方法准确率高，成本低。

Conclusion: 该算法有效实现了双语词典的词性标注，为资源匮乏语言的NLP工具开发提供了轻资源方案。

Abstract: This paper proposed an algorithm for part-of-speech (POS) tagging senses of a bilingual dictionary. The algorithm is applied on the Al-Mawrid Arabic-English dictionary. The tagging task is accomplished by transferring the POS tags of the English translation equivalences (TEs) to the dictionary senses after dis-ambiguities process. The English POS tags of senses are acquired from the Princeton WordNet. POS tagging of bilingual dictionary senses is prerequisite to link a bilingual dictionary to WordNet and/or standardizing that dictionary into WordNet-LMF format where the synset (set of synonyms), not word, is the basic brick. The registered accuracy is high though the cost is little. Building NLP/HLT tools needs linguistic experts, large investments, and long time. For statistical approach, we need large annotated corpora and for rule-based approach, we need large lexicon that contains rich linguistic and world knowledge. That motivates the appearance of what are called resource-light approaches to develop natural language processing (NLP) tools for poor-resource languages.

</details>


### [39] [MorfFlex: Handling Rich Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24366)
*Jaroslava Hlaváčová,Marie Mikulová,Barbora Štěpánková,Milan Straka,Jan Hajič*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MorfFlex——一种适用于高度规则性屈折和派生语言的形态词典架构，并以捷克语形态词典MorfFlex CZ为例进行说明。该词典通过手工维护的源文件和转换脚本编码了复杂的屈折和派生模式，从而将词典规模从原本可能巨大的体量压缩至当前包含超过1亿词形和100万词条的规模。MorfFlex CZ是保证布拉格依存树库中手工形态标注一致性、并支撑MorphoDiTa等先进工具的关键资源。本文重点展示了管理丰富形态系统的有效方法，以及此类语言资源在维护语料库标注一致性和支持高级NLP应用开发中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具有丰富形态变化的语言（如捷克语），构建大规模且一致性高的形态词典面临巨大挑战。传统方法要么难以覆盖所有词形，要么词典规模过于庞大难以维护。迫切需要一种既能有效管理复杂形态系统、又能保持合理规模的词典架构。

Method: 采用MorfFlex架构，以简单的<词形, 词条, 标签>三元组列表形式分发词典，但通过手工维护的源文件和转换脚本编码复杂的屈折和派生模式，利用这些模式将词典规模大幅压缩。捷克语词典MorfFlex CZ基于该架构实现。

Result: MorfFlex CZ词典目前包含超过1亿词形和100万以上词条，在保持高度覆盖的同时通过模式化编码控制了词典规模。该词典成功保证了布拉格依存树库中手工形态标注的一致性，并成为MorphoDiTa等先进工具的基础资源。

Conclusion: MorfFlex架构为高度规则性屈折和派生语言提供了有效的形态词典管理方案。此类语言资源对于维护大规模语料库中手工标注的一致性和推动先进NLP应用开发具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present MorfFlex, a morphological dictionary architecture suitable for languages with extensive regularity in both inflection and derivation. As the primary example of MorfFlex in use we introduce MorfFlex CZ, a morphological dictionary of Czech. It is distributed as a simple, unstructured list of <wordform, lemma, tag> triplets, however, its manually maintained, unpublished source files and conversion scripts encode a sophisticated system of inflectional and derivational patterns. These patterns dramatically reduce the otherwise enormous size of the dictionary, which currently contains over 100 million wordforms and more than 1 million lemmas. The MorfFlex CZ dictionary serves as an essential resource for ensuring the consistency of manual morphological annotation in the Prague Dependency Treebanks and underpins state-of-the-art automatic tools such as MorphoDiTa. In this paper, we focus on: (i) presenting an effective method for managing the rich morphological system within the dictionary, and (ii) demonstrating the utility of such a language resource for maintaining annotation consistency in corpora and supporting the development of advanced NLP applications.

</details>


### [40] [On the Stability of Prompt Ranking in Large Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24381)
*Shaoshuai Du,Penghao Liang,Yixian Shen,Chuanqi Shi,Hang Zhang,Lun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM提示排名在随机种子和有限评估子集下的不稳定性，发现虽然总体排名相关性中等至高，但最佳提示的身份经常变化，导致选择不可靠。作者提出基于置信下界的稳定感知选择策略，在提高鲁棒性的同时保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于提示的LLM使用范式隐式假设提示排名在评估条件微小变化下是稳定的，但这一假设尚未被系统验证。

Method: 在三种开源LLM和两个基准任务上，系统评估随机种子和有限评估子集对提示排名稳定性的影响；提出基于置信下界的稳定感知选择策略。

Result: 总体排名相关性中等至较高，但最佳提示的身份经常变化导致选择不可靠；提出的策略在非稳定设置下提升了鲁棒性，在稳定设置下保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 评估不确定性在提示选择和LLM基准测试中具有重要意义，应被纳入考量。

Abstract: Prompt-based interaction has become a dominant paradigm for using large language models (LLMs), where multiple candidate prompts are evaluated and the top-ranked one is selected for downstream use. This workflow implicitly assumes that prompt rankings are stable under minor variations in evaluation conditions. In this paper, we systematically study prompt ranking stability under common sources of variability, including random seeds and limited evaluation subsets. Across three open-weight LLMs and two benchmark tasks, we find that while overall rank correlations are often moderate to high, the identity of the top-performing prompt frequently changes, leading to unreliable selection decisions. To address this issue, we propose a simple stability-aware selection strategy based on a lower confidence bound, which accounts for both performance and variance. Our results show that this approach improves robustness in unstable settings while remaining competitive in more stable regimes. These findings highlight the importance of accounting for evaluation uncertainty in prompt selection and LLM benchmarking.

</details>


### [41] [AutoSpecNER: A Fine-Grained Named Entity Recognition Dataset for Vehicle Specification Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24387)
*Jordan Lee,Filippos Ventirozos,Abdirahman Abdullahm,Ioanna Nteka,Peter Appleby,Matthew Shardlow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoSpecNER是一个专家标注的车辆广告细粒度实体识别数据集，包含659条广告、10,000+实体（15个类别），标注一致性达91.5%。在此基准测试中，DeBERTa以90%的micro-F1分数表现最佳，远超基于规则的基线（43%）和最强大型语言模型（77.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆广告包含丰富的规格信息，但面向汽车领域的命名实体识别（NER）资源有限，缺乏高质量的标注数据集。

Method: 构建了AutoSpecNER数据集：从流行汽车销售网站收集659条广告，由专家标注超过10,000个实体（15个类别），并通过标注者间一致性（91.5%）验证质量。基准测试涵盖基于规则的方法、微调Transformer编码器（DeBERTa）和大型语言模型。

Result: DeBERTa取得最佳性能，micro-F1分数达90%；规则基线为43%；最强LLM为77.8%。

Conclusion: 在汽车NER任务中，微调DeBERTa显著优于规则方法和大型语言模型，表明专用微调模型在此细粒度实体识别场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Vehicle advertisements contain rich specification information, but automotive NER resources remain limited. We introduce AutoSpecNER, an expert-annotated dataset for fine-grained entity recognition in vehicle listings. The dataset includes 659 advertisements from a popular car-selling website, with over 10,000 entities annotated across 15 categories, including MODEL, ENGINE_SPEC, and BATTERY_CAPACITY. Annotation quality was validated through inter-annotator agreement, achieving an average score of 91.5%. We benchmark rule-based extraction, fine-tuned transformer encoders, and large language models. DeBERTa achieves the best performance with a 90% micro-F1 score, outperforming the rule-based baseline (43%) and the strongest large language model (77.8%).

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Logprobs: A Multi-Signal Confidence Engine for LLM-Based Document Field Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24420)
*Nitesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ExtractConf 是一种跨领域、字段无关的置信度评估引擎，通过两次结构不同的文档读取（Hunter 字段引导抽取与 Mapper 文档引导扫描）产生的分歧信号，结合 LLM 内部不确定性、OCR、图像质量和空间布局等多维特征，在无需领域规则或重训练的条件下实现高可靠的抽取置信度评估，在 DocILE 和 CORD 数据集上显著优于传统的 logprob 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险文档处理流水线（如财务对账、合规验证、采购自动化）中，LLM 抽取结果"沉默地错误"比"可见地缺失"危害更大。核心挑战并非抽取精度本身，而是可靠的置信度估计——逐字段判断抽取结果是否可信任以自动化处理，还是应移交人工审核。现有方法（token 级 log-probabilities、口头化置信度、多样本自一致性）在实际阈值下均坍缩为全阳性行为，无法可靠分离可信与不可信的抽取结果。

Method: ExtractConf 包含两次结构不同的文档读取：(1) Hunter——字段引导抽取，在 schema 槽位完成压力下抽取每个字段；(2) Mapper——文档引导扫描，整体性地扫描并输出基于文档内容的值。两次读取的不对称性产生不同的失败模式（Hunter 为缺失字段产生幻觉值，Mapper 漏掉视觉非显著值），其分歧具有独立信息量。ExtractConf 融合跨调用分歧、LLM 内部不确定性、OCR、图像质量和空间布局等多维特征，通过分类器进行置信度评估，无需领域特定规则或重训练。

Result: 在 DocILE 数据集（55字段发票，26%失败率）上，ROC AUC 达 0.928，选择性预测风险较 logprob-mean 降低 70%；80%覆盖率下准确率达 99.1%。零样本迁移到 CORD 收据数据集 AUC 达 0.858；轻量 Lasso 重校准将 ECE 降低 89%、Brier 分数降低 43%。

Conclusion: 通过两次结构不同读取的分歧信号能够有效估计 LLM 文档抽取的置信度，且该信号跨文档领域具有泛化能力，仅需轻量校准即可迁移到新领域，为高风险自动化场景提供了实用的人工复核工作流基础。

Abstract: In high-stakes document processing pipelines, including financial reconciliation, compliance verification, and procurement automation, an LLM extraction that is silently wrong is more dangerous than one that is visibly absent. The central challenge is not extraction accuracy alone but reliable confidence estimation: knowing, field by field, whether an extraction can be trusted for automation or deferred to human review. Token-level log-probabilities, verbalized confidence, and multi-sample self-consistency all collapse toward all-positive behaviour at practical thresholds, offering no reliable separation between trustworthy and untrustworthy extractions.
  We present ExtractConf, a cross-domain, field-agnostic confidence engine that grounds confidence estimation in two structurally different readings of the same document. A field-guided Hunter call extracts each field under schema-slot completion pressure; a document-guided Mapper call scans holistically and surfaces values grounded in document content. This asymmetry yields different failure modes: Hunter hallucinates values for absent fields, while Mapper misses visually non-salient ones. Their disagreement is independently informative. ExtractConf fuses cross-call disagreement, LLM-internal uncertainty, OCR, image quality, and spatial layout into a classifier requiring no domain-specific rules or retraining. On DocILE (55-field invoices, 26% failure rate), it achieves 0.928 ROC AUC and reduces selective prediction risk by 70% over logprob-mean. At 80% coverage, accuracy reaches 99.1%, enabling a practical human-in-the-loop workflow. Zero-shot transfer to CORD receipts achieves 0.858 AUC; lightweight Lasso recalibration reduces ECE by 89% and Brier by 43%, confirming the signals generalise across document domains.

</details>


### [43] [Escaping the Self-Confirmation Trap: An Execute-Distill-Verify Paradigm for Agentic Experience Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24428)
*Shiding Zhu,Yudi Qi,Yajie Wang,Jiaze Li,Chao Song,Yaorui Shi,Yibo Miao,Hanqi Gao,Kai Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EDV（执行-蒸馏-验证）框架，通过多智能体协作替代单智能体自我反思，解决LLM智能体经验学习中的自我确认陷阱问题，在三个长程基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验学习方法依赖单智能体循环，导致自我确认陷阱——错误但自洽的轨迹被误判为成功经验，造成检索和复用时的累积误差。

Method: EDV框架包含三个阶段：Execute阶段（多个异构智能体并行探索任务空间生成候选轨迹）、Distill阶段（第三方智能体对比分析轨迹生成候选经验）、Verify阶段（执行组通过共识机制验证，仅批准的经验写入共享或私有记忆）。

Result: 在tau2-bench、Mind2Web和MMTB三个长程基准上，EDV一致优于强基线方法，验证了可靠经验构建对智能体自我进化的重要性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦执行、蒸馏、验证三个阶段，将经验学习从孤立自我反思转变为协同构建，在记忆插入前过滤错误和噪声内容，有效提升LLM智能体自我进化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Experience-driven self-evolution is critical for large language model (LLM) agents to improve through open-world interaction. However, existing experience learning methods mostly rely on single-agent loops, where the same agent executes tasks, summarizes outcomes, and determines memory content. This setup makes agents vulnerable to the Self-Confirmation Trap: wrong-but-self-consistent trajectories are misidentified as successful experience, leading to cumulative errors during retrieval and reuse. To address this issue, we propose EDV, an Execute-Distill-Verify framework for reliable experience learning. In the Execute stage, multiple heterogeneous agents explore the same task space in parallel to generate diverse candidate trajectories. In the Distill stage, a dedicated third-party agent comparatively analyzes these trajectories to produce candidate experiences, reducing executor-centric summarization bias. In the Verify stage, the execution group validates candidates via a consensus mechanism, and only approved experiences are written into shared or private memory. By decoupling the three stages, EDV transforms experience learning from isolated self-reflection into collaborative construction, filtering erroneous and noisy content before memory insertion. We evaluate EDV on three challenging long-horizon benchmarks: tau2-bench, Mind2Web and MMTB. Results show EDV consistently outperforms strong baselines, validating that reliable experience construction is essential for robust agent self-evolution. Our code is available at https://github.com/shidingz/EDV.

</details>


### [44] [The African Language Tax: Quantifying the Cost, Latency, and Context Penalty of Tokenizing African Languages in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24460)
*Olaoye Anthony Somide*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 非洲语言在LLM分词中承受结构性惩罚：相比英语，其同等语义需要更多子词单元，导致最高8.9倍推理成本、最低11%有效上下文窗口，且现有分词器无法消除该差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知多语言分词存在token fertility差异，但此前缺乏针对非洲语言在企业部署经济学和认知上下文容量层面的大规模系统测量。

Method: 使用平行语料（FLORES-200+和MAFAND-MT），对20种非洲语言（涵盖5个语系、3种文字系统）在11个前沿和开放分词器上进行token fertility测量，隔离内容效应。

Result: 所有非洲语言均存在分词溢价（中位数1.88x，最高8.92x），Ethiopic和N'Ko文字惩罚最大（7-9x），跨语料库近不变（Pearson r=0.9998）。转化为8.9x推理成本、等效生成延迟乘数，有效上下文窗口仅为英语的11%。最佳分词器Gemma 4仅将平均溢价从3.31x降至2.38x。

Conclusion: 分词器在子词词汇层面直接编码了数字鸿沟，对最无力承担的语言用户惩罚最大。研究发布了开放测量工具和缓解指南。

Abstract: Commercial large language models bill, scale latency, and budget context per token. Yet tokenizers assign more subword tokens to the same meaning in some languages than in others, so speakers of languages with high token-fertility pay a structural penalty before a model is ever invoked. This penalty is documented for multilingual settings in general, but it has not been measured systematically for African languages at the level of enterprise deployment economics and cognitive context capacity. We measure it across 20 African languages spanning five language families and three scripts (Latin, Ge'ez/Ethiopic, N'Ko; 19 appear in the primary FLORES-200+ corpus, with Nigerian Pidgin measured via MAFAND-MT only), using parallel corpora so that the language effect is isolated from content. Across 11 frontier and open tokenizers on FLORES-200+, every African language carries a tokenization premium above English (median 1.88x on GPT-5 / o200k_base, up to 8.92x for N'Ko); the penalty is largest for Ethiopic and N'Ko scripts (reaching 7-9x) and is near-invariant across corpora (FLORES vs SIB-200 Pearson r = 0.9998). Translated into deployment terms, this results in up to 8.9x inference cost and an equivalent generation-latency multiplier (N'Ko vs English on GPT-5; 7.4x for Amharic), and as little as 11% of English's effective context window. The best currently available tokenizer for African languages, Gemma 4, reduces the mean premium from 3.31x (cl100k_base) to 2.38x, but no tokenizer eliminates the penalty. We release an open measurement tool (afri-fertility), a public leaderboard, a results dataset, and mitigation guidance for African builders. The penalty falls hardest on the languages whose speakers can least afford it, a digital divide encoded directly into the subword vocabulary.

</details>

*Nouran Khallaf,Serge Sharoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于回归的多语言词汇难度预测系统，结合多语言上下文表示与多种工程特征（频率、形态、检索、语义对齐、同源相似性、MLM可预测性），在BEA 2026共享任务中针对西班牙语、德语和汉语取得了优于基线的结果，其中频率是最稳定的预测因子，但模型对最简单词项存在过度预测的校准问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇难度预测是自然语言处理中的重要任务，现有方法多针对单一语言，缺乏L1感知的多语言词汇难度预测系统。本文旨在开发一个能同时处理西班牙语、德语和汉语的词汇难度预测系统，并探索不同特征对预测效果的贡献。

Method: 将任务建模为回归问题，为每种语言训练独立系统。采用多语言上下文表示（BGE-M3、mE5、LaBSE等句子嵌入编码器）与手工特征相结合的方式，手工特征包括：词频、表面形式、检索证据、语义对齐、同源相似性和掩码语言模型可预测性。

Result: 官方提交的RMSE分数：西班牙语1.132，德语1.037，汉语0.891。频率是最稳定的预测因子，上下文可预测性、形式相似性、检索和语义特征提供了互补的L1敏感信号。模型在排名性能上表现良好，但对最简单词项存在校准不足（过度预测）的问题。

Conclusion: 多语言上下文表示结合工程特征能有效预测L1感知的词汇难度，其中句子嵌入编码器（BGE-M3、mE5、LaBSE）表现最佳。未来可针对简单词项的校准问题进行改进。

Abstract: This paper describes UOL@IDEM's closed-track submission to the BEA 2026 shared task on L1-aware vocabulary difficulty prediction. We model the task as regression and train separate systems for Spanish, German, and Mandarin Chinese\footnote{Below we use \emph{Chinese} for brevity.}. Our system combines multilingual contextual representations with engineered features capturing frequency, surface form, retrieval evidence, semantic alignment, cognate similarity, and masked-language-model predictability. Development results show consistent gains over the official closed-track baselines, with sentence-embedding encoders such as BGE-M3, multilingual E5, and LaBSE performing best. Official submissions achieve RMSE scores of 1.132, 1.037, and 0.891 for Spanish, German, and Chinese, respectively. Feature analysis identifies frequency as the most stable predictor, while contextual predictability, form similarity, retrieval, and semantic features provide complementary L1-sensitive signals. Error analysis shows strong ranking performance but weaker calibration for the easiest items, which are often overpredicted. See https://github.com/Nouran-Khallaf/UoL-IDEM-BEA2026-Vocabulary-Difficulty-Prediction

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 诈骗电话在全球范围内危害弱势群体，但目前检测研究几乎全部集中在英语等资源丰富的语言上。在土耳其语等低资源环境下，标注数据稀缺、技术防御有限，亟需探索LLM在此场景下的检测能力。

Conclusion: 本研究突显了在文化和语言层面包容性的AI安全研究的紧迫性，以及构建更强健的多模态欺诈预防系统的必要性。

Abstract: Scam phone calls exploit vulnerable communities worldwide, yet research on detection has focused almost exclusively on English and other high-resource languages. In low-resource settings such as Turkish, detection is especially difficult, as annotated data is scarce and technological defenses remain limited. This research investigates how large language models (LLMs) can support scam detection in Turkish by introducing the first public multi-modal dataset of 100 aligned audio-transcript pairs of scam and benign conversations. We evaluate seven LLMs spanning three model families: Gemini 2.5 (Flash, Flash-Lite, Pro), GPT-4o, and Qwen (Max, Plus, Turbo), under three input conditions: raw audio, automatic speech-to-text transcripts, and transcripts refined by a native speaker. Our results suggest that transcript-based inputs consistently outperform direct audio processing, while human-corrected and uncorrected transcripts perform comparably. By centering a low-resource language and real world threat, this work highlights the urgent need for culturally and linguistically inclusive AI safety research and more robust multi-modal systems for fraud prevention.

</details>

*Honglin Guo,Qi Zhang,Yu Zhang,Weijie Li,Rui Zheng,Zhikai Lei,Qiyuan Peng,Zhiheng Xi,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agora基准测试，包含362个问题、8个领域的9,664份真实文档（3.72亿token），用于评估大语言模型在存档文档中基于证据进行推理的能力。当前最强模型仅达到59.4%的准确率，任务远未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅覆盖存档文档推理的部分方面，缺乏同时强调存档基础、智能体探索和跨领域覆盖的评测，因此需要更全面的基准来评估LLM在此类任务上的表现。

Method: 构建Agora基准：从8个领域收集9,664份真实文档（372M tokens），生成362个需要跨文档定位稀疏证据、协调不一致信息的问题；采用智能体管线进行跨文档任务合成、防泄漏混淆和难度过滤。

Conclusion: 存档文档推理任务极具挑战性，当前LLM在该场景下能力有限，需要进一步研究以提升模型在真实工作文档环境中进行智能体式探索和推理的能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as agents that reason over documents rather than answer from parametric knowledge. We study archive-grounded reasoning: locating sparse evidence across a large, messy collection of workplace files, reconciling inconsistent terminology, units, and time conventions, and computing an answer. Existing benchmarks address only parts of this setting and none jointly stresses archive-groundedness, agentic exploration, and cross-domain coverage. We introduce Agora, a benchmark pairing 362 questions with eight domain collections of 9,664 authentic documents and 372M tokens, far exceeding any model's context window, so agents must explore deliberately rather than scan exhaustively. Agora is built by an agentic pipeline combining cross-document task synthesis, leakage-preventing obfuscation, and difficulty filtering. Evaluating eight models, we find the task far from solved: even the strongest reaches only 59.4% accuracy, with notable variation across domains.

</details>


### [48] [NatureBench: Can Coding Agents Match the Published SOTA of Nature-Family Papers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24530)
*Yuru Wang,Lejun Cheng,Yuxin Zuo,Sihang Zeng,Bingxiang He,Che Jiang,Junlin Yang,Yuchong Wang,Kaikai Zhao,Weifeng Huang,Kai Tian,Zhenzhao Yuan,Jincheng Zhong,Weizhi Wang,Ning Ding,Bowen Zhou,Kaiyan Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法可信地评估AI代理在真实科学研究中的表现，主要受限于环境碎片化问题。作者希望建立一个标准化的、跨学科的科学问题基准，测试AI代理是否能从复现迈向真正的科学发现。

Method: 从Nature系列同行评审论文中蒸馏出90个任务，通过NatureGym自动化流水线为每个任务构建标准化的容器化环境，解决了环境碎片化问题。在严格的禁用网络搜索协议下评估了十种前沿代理配置，并分析了代理的方法路径和失败原因。

Result: 最强模型在g>0.1标准下仅17.8%的任务超越SOTA。代理主要通过方法论翻译（将科学任务转化为熟悉的监督预测问题）成功，而非科学发明。失败主要源于错误的方法选择和计算预算不足，而非任务理解错误。

Conclusion: 当前AI编码代理在真实科学发现任务上表现有限，主要瓶颈在于方法选择和计算资源，而非任务理解。NatureBench和NatureGym为评估AI驱动的科学发现提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: We introduce NatureBench, a cross-discipline benchmark of 90 tasks distilled from peer-reviewed Nature-family publications, designed to evaluate whether AI coding agents can move beyond reproduction toward discovery on real scientific problems. NatureBench is built on NatureGym, an automated pipeline that constructs a standardized, per-task containerized environment from a source paper, addressing the environment-fragmentation problem that has limited the credibility of prior agent-on-research benchmarks. Evaluating ten frontier agent configurations under a strict web-search-disabled protocol, we find that the strongest model surpasses SOTA on only 17.8% of tasks under the g>0.1 criterion. Analysis of method pathways reveals that agents succeed primarily through methodological translation, converting scientific tasks into familiar supervised prediction problems, rather than through genuine scientific invention. Failures are dominated by wrong method choice and insufficient compute budget, not by task misunderstanding. We release the benchmark, the NatureGym pipeline, and a public leaderboard with maintainer-side reproduction. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/NatureBench

</details>


### [49] [Cross-Lingual Exploration for Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24579)
*Elisha Diskind,Itamar Trainin,Uri Shaham,Leshem Choshen,Idan Szpektor,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究跨语言提示探索策略，通过识别影响参数化知识检索的四个内在维度，在17种语言的多语言事实基准上验证了该方法相比原生语言扩展更高效地提升了知识迁移和事实召回能力，并改善了跨语言一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中的参数化知识在不同语言间不可等同访问，标准推理方法难以挖掘本地化事实，导致跨语言知识迁移和一致性失败。

Method: 识别了跨语言探索的四个内在维度，并设计跨语言提示策略，在涵盖17种类型学多样语言的多语言事实基准上进行评估。

Result: 跨语言探索显著提升了知识迁移和事实召回，在计算效率上优于原生语言扩展的Pareto前沿；同时跨语言一致性得到改善，其提升幅度超越了仅由准确率增益可解释的范围。

Conclusion: 多语言提示探索是一种高效的推理时策略，能够有效解锁大型语言模型中潜在的参数化知识。

Abstract: Parametric knowledge in Large Language Models is not equally accessible across languages. As a result, standard inference techniques often struggle to surface localized facts, leading to failures in cross-lingual knowledge transfer and consistency. In this work, we investigate techniques for accessing hidden factual knowledge by exploring cross-lingual prompting strategies. We identify four inherent dimensions of cross-lingual exploration that directly govern parametric knowledge retrieval and evaluate them on multilingual factual benchmarks covering 17 typologically diverse languages. Our results demonstrate that cross-lingual exploration significantly improves knowledge transfer and factual recall, representing a more efficient compute Pareto frontier than native-language scaling. Furthermore, we observe corresponding improvements in cross-lingual consistency, exceeding what can be explained by accuracy gains alone. Overall, our work establishes multilingual prompt exploration as a highly effective inference-time strategy for unlocking latent parametric knowledge.

</details>

*Enze Ma,Yufan Zhou,Wei-Chieh Huang,Jie Yang,Huanhuan Ma,Zixuan Wang,Chengze Li,Chunyu Miao,Philip S. Yu,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MEMPROBE基准，通过从智能体记忆痕迹中重建用户状态（而非仅依赖下游任务表现）来评估大语言模型智能体的长期记忆能力。实验表明任务完成与记忆可恢复性是两种独立能力，当前最先进智能体在记忆恢复上表现中等（约0.6），且受到top-k检索的显著限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆评估主要通过下游行为（如答案质量、个性化效果）间接测试，缺乏对记忆工件本身的审计。本文主张应将长期记忆作为可审计的后交互工件来评估，即从智能体留下的记忆痕迹中重建用户结构化状态。

Method: 构建MEMPROBE基准：50个模拟用户，每个携带31个隐藏维度（共1,550个恢复目标），基于合成真值实现高效可扩展测量；智能体在泄露控制的任务轨迹中辅助用户，之后从记忆中进行全库和top-k访问下的用户状态重建；测试5种代表性记忆系统。

Result: 任务完成率几乎饱和（即使无记忆基线），但类别平衡的记忆恢复率仅约0.6，且top-k检索下进一步下降。成功辅助与可恢复记忆表现为两种不同能力。

Conclusion: MEMPROBE是首个直接研究记忆恢复的基准，将用户状态重建作为未来记忆智能体可优化的具体目标，推动智能体在更长时间互动中形成更忠实的用户记忆。

Abstract: Long-term memory promises LLM agents that grow more capable across sessions, maintaining an accurate, evolving understanding of the user that interaction forms. In practice, however, this memory is evaluated mostly through downstream behavior, such as later answers, personalization quality, or task success, which tests that understanding only indirectly and leaves the memory artifact itself largely unaudited. We argue that long-term memory should instead be evaluated as an auditable post-interaction artifact: after ordinary assistance, what structured user state can be reconstructed from the memory the agent leaves behind? We instantiate this view in MEMPROBE, a benchmark in which a memory-equipped agent assists simulated users, each carrying a hidden, taxonomy-anchored user-state bank, across a trajectory of leak-controlled tasks, after which that bank is reconstructed from the agent's resulting memory under both full-store and top-k access. Built on synthetic ground truth for efficient, scalable measurement, MEMPROBE spans 50 simulated users with 31 hidden dimensions each (1,550 recovery targets) and tests 5 representative memory systems. Testing state-of-the-art memory agents, we find that successful assistance and recoverable memory behave as distinct capabilities. Task completion nearly saturates, even for a memoryless baseline, while category-balanced recovery stays moderate (about 0.6) and drops further under top-k retrieval. MEMPROBE is the first benchmark to study memory recovery directly, reconstructing the user state a system retains and scoring it against ground truth. We see recovery as a concrete objective for future memory agents to optimize, and MEMPROBE as a step toward an environment where agents are trained to remember their users, growing more faithful the longer they know them.

</details>


### [51] [To Compare, or Not to Compare: On Methodological Practices in Evaluating Social Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24596)
*Federico Marcuzzi,Xuefei Ning,Roy Schwartz,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出统一可控的评估框架，揭示了社交偏见评估中存在系统性范式鸿沟：孤立评估抑制偏见激活，而对比设置会催化潜在的歧视行为，该现象随模型规模扩大而增强，且CoT推理和中性选项均无法消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM社交偏见评估存在严重的方法碎片化问题，导致结论相互矛盾，根本原因是忽略了评估基准的结构性框架设计。

Method: 构建统一可控框架，标准化异构基准，系统对比孤立人口统计学评估与强制选择比较设置，分离CoT推理、中性选项等结构伪影的混淆效应。

Result: 发现了大规模、系统性的范式鸿沟：对比设置会催化潜在歧视；CoT在对比设置下加剧偏见；提供中性选项或声称随机回答后偏见仍作为确定性偏见持续存在；比较偏见随模型规模正向扩展。

Conclusion: 研究者应利用对比设置来稳健审计隐藏偏见，但实践者不能在模糊真实任务中安全地依赖对比部署。

Abstract: As Large Language Models are increasingly deployed in critical applications, robustly evaluating their social biases is paramount. However, the current literature suffers from widespread methodological fragmentation, which yields contradictory conclusions. This stems largely from ignoring the structural framing of benchmark-level evaluations. To resolve this, we introduce a unified and controllable framework that standardizes heterogeneous benchmarks to systematically contrast isolated demographic assessments with forced-choice comparative settings. Crucially, this allows us to disentangle the confounding effects of Chain-of-Thought reasoning, neutral fallback options, and other structural artifacts in social bias evaluations. Our evaluation across multiple model families reveals a massive, systematic paradigm gap: while isolated assessments limit prejudice activation, comparative settings act as aggressive catalysts for latent discrimination, a shift primarily driven by underspecified contexts. Alarmingly, CoT reasoning exacerbates social biases under comparative settings, and this systemic bias persists as a deterministic prejudice even when models are provided neutral fallback options or claim to answer randomly. Finally, we demonstrate that this comparative prejudice is a generalized phenomenon that scales positively with model size. Ultimately, we offer a crucial methodological guideline: while researchers must leverage comparative settings to robustly audit hidden biases, practitioners cannot safely rely on comparative deployments in ambiguous real-world tasks.

</details>


### [52] [Qwen-AgentWorld: Language World Models for General Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24597)
*Yuxin Zuo,Zikai Xiao,Li Sheng,Fei Huang,Jianhong Tu,Yuxuan Liu,Tianyi Tang,Xiaomeng Hu,Yang Su,Qingfeng Lan,Yantao Liu,Qin Zhu,Yinger Zhang,Bowen Yu,Haiquan Zhao,Haiyang Xu,Jianxin Yang,Jiayang Cheng,Junyang Wang,Lianghao Deng,Mingfeng Xue,Tianyi Bai,Yang Fan,Yubo Ma,Yucheng Li,Zeyu Cui,Zhihai Wang,Zhihui Xie,Zhuorui Ye,An Yang,Dayiheng Liu,Jingren Zhou,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于语言模型的世界模型Qwen-AgentWorld，涵盖35B-A3B和397B-A17B两个规模，通过三阶段训练（CPT、SFT、RL）在7个领域、1000万+真实交互轨迹上训练，能够模拟智能体环境。同时提出AgentWorldBench评估基准，并探索了世界模型作为解耦环境模拟器和统一智能体基础模型的两种范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于语言模型的世界建模如何进一步推动通用智能体的能力边界，构建能够模拟智能体环境的语言世界模型。

Method: 三阶段训练流水线：CPT注入通用世界建模能力（基于状态转移动力学和增强专业语料），SFT激活下一状态预测推理，RL通过混合量规与规则奖励的定制框架提升仿真保真度。模型在7个领域、1000万+真实环境交互轨迹上训练。

Result: Qwen-AgentWorld在AgentWorldBench上显著优于现有前沿模型；作为解耦环境模拟器可支持数千真实环境的可扩展仿真，超越纯真实环境训练；作为统一智能体基础模型，世界模型训练作为高效预热可提升7个智能体基准的下游性能。

Conclusion: 语言世界模型能够有效模拟多领域智能体环境，通过三阶段训练和混合奖励机制实现高保真仿真，并可在环境模拟和智能体基础模型两种范式中提升通用智能体能力。

Abstract: A world model predicts environment dynamics based on current observations and actions, serving as a core cognitive mechanism for reasoning and planning. In this work, we investigate how world modeling based on language models can further push the boundaries of general agents. (i) We first focus on building foundation models for agentic environment simulation. We introduce Qwen-AgentWorld-35B-A3B and Qwen-AgentWorld-397B-A17B, the first language world models capable of simulating agentic environments covering 7 domains via long chain-of-thought reasoning. Leveraging more than 10M environment interaction trajectories of 7 domains in real-world environments, we develop Qwen-AgentWorld through a three-stage training pipeline: CPT injects general-purpose world modeling capabilities from the state transition dynamics and augmented professional corpora, SFT activates next-state-prediction reasoning, and RL sharpens simulation fidelity through a tailored framework with hybrid rubric-and-rule rewards. To evaluate language world models, we present AgentWorldBench, a comprehensive benchmark constructed from real-world interactions of 5 frontier models on 9 established benchmarks. Empirical results demonstrate that Qwen-AgentWorld significantly outperforms existing frontier models. (ii) Beyond foundation models, we further investigate two complementary paradigms through which world modeling enhances general agents. First, as a decoupled environment simulator, Qwen-AgentWorld supports scalable and controllable simulation of thousands of real-world environments for agentic RL, yielding gains that surpass real-environment training alone. Second, as a unified agent foundation model, world-model training acts as a highly effective warm-up that improves downstream performance across 7 agentic benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/QwenLM/Qwen-AgentWorld

</details>


### [53] [Same Lesson, Different Story: Cross-Lingual Reconstruction of Cultural Narratives in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24610)
*Jory Alshaalan,Haya Albaker,Abeer Aldayel,Aljawharah Alabdullatif,Rehab Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个多语言叙事评估框架，利用15种语言的414条谚语和4个LLM生成的13k叙事，分析模型在不同语言和文化背景下是否保留文化扎根意义。结果显示跨语言提示能保留谚语语义，但会系统性地改变叙事中的施动性、社会定位和结构，且不同模型间存在强收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多种文化传达相同道德教训时，文化扎根语境的评估变得复杂。LLM在不同语言和文化中生成叙事，但尚不确定当等效道德教训通过不同文化形式传达时，模型是否保留文化扎根意义。

Method: 构建跨语言谚语数据集（15种语言、414条语义等效谚语），使用4种LLM生成13k叙事，通过跨语言提示分析语义保留、叙事结构变化及模型间收敛性。

Result: 跨语言提示大致保留谚语语义，但系统性地重新分布施动性、社会定位和叙事结构；单语和跨语言设置中均观察到强模型间收敛性。

Conclusion: 多语言LLM依赖共享语义抽象，但仅依靠语义相似性评估多语言叙事可能高估文化保留程度，忽略了叙事表达中文化意义上的变异。

Abstract: The evaluation of cultural grounding context becomes complex when multiple cultures convey the same moral lesson. This challenge is particularly relevant to large language models (LLMs), which produce narratives across a wide range of languages and cultural contexts. However, it remains uncertain whether these models preserve culturally grounded meaning when equivalent moral lessons are conveyed through distinct cultural forms. This study introduces a multilingual evaluation narrative framework that integrates a cross-linguistic collection of 414 proverbs spanning 15 languages and uses four LLMs to generate 13k narratives. By employing semantically equivalent proverbs as culturally grounded prompts, the analysis assesses whether models preserve meaning across languages, how cross-lingual conditioning influences narrative realization, and whether different model families converge on similar interpretations. Results indicate that cross-lingual prompting largely preserves proverb-level semantic meaning while systematically redistributing agency, social positioning, and narrative structure. Additionally, strong inter-model convergence is observed in both monolingual and cross-lingual settings, suggesting that multilingual LLMs rely on shared semantic abstractions despite architectural and linguistic differences. These findings shed light on the need for more comprehensive evaluations of cultural grounding. Relying exclusively on semantic similarity in multilingual narrative assessments may overestimate cultural preservation by neglecting culturally meaningful variations in narrative expression.

</details>


### [54] [Privacy-Preserving RAG via Multi-Agent Semantic Rewriting: Achieving Confidentiality Without Compromising Contextual Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24623)
*Yuanhe Zhao,Tianyu Zhang,Huafei Xing,Derek F. Wong,Jianbin Li,Tao Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一个多智能体框架，通过语义重写对检索内容进行隐私脱敏处理，在保持语义核心的同时去除敏感标识符，显著降低RAG系统中的隐私泄露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在敏感场景中部署检索增强生成（RAG）系统面临隐私泄露风险，恶意提示可能诱使模型泄露检索内容中的敏感信息。

Method: 构建一个多智能体框架，包含三个专用智能体：隐私提取（识别敏感信息）、语义分析（分析上下文语义）、重建（去除敏感标识符并重构内容），通过协作实现检索内容的语义重写。

Result: 在ChatDoctor和Wiki-PII数据集上，跨6个大语言模型评估，显著降低隐私泄露（例如LLaMA-3-8B上目标信息暴露从144次降至1次），BLEU-1得分为0.122，优于SAGE方法的0.117。

Conclusion: 该框架作为异步预处理模块运行，所有重写为一次性离线预处理步骤，不增加在线推理延迟，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation enhances large language models by incorporating external knowledge, but deploying it in sensitive scenarios risks privacy leakage via malicious prompts. To address this, we propose a multi-agent framework that sanitizes retrieved content through semantic rewriting. By employing three specialized agents for privacy extraction, semantic analysis, and reconstruction, our approach collaboratively removes sensitive identifiers while preserving the semantic core. We evaluate the framework on the ChatDoctor and Wiki-PII datasets across six large language models. Experimental results demonstrate a significant reduction in privacy leakage under targeted attacks. For instance, we reduced targeted information exposure in LLaMA-3-8B from 144 instances in the baseline to just 1. Furthermore, we maintain strong contextual fidelity with a BLEU-1 score of 0.122, outperforming the existing SAGE method's 0.117. Finally, the framework operates as an asynchronous preprocessing module, introducing no additional latency to online inference, as all rewriting is executed as a one-time offline preprocessing step. To promote reproducibility, the source code of this work is publicly available at https://github.com/foursoils/Privacy-Preserving-RAG.

</details>


### [55] [The Warrant Gap: Claim-Conditioned Re-scoring for Fact-Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24627)
*Arka Ujjal Dey,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SIFT方法（基于条件重评分的证据提取）和WSP指标（自动NLI验证），用于改善事实验证系统中LLM生成的支持标签与其引用证据之间的逻辑一致性。在多个基准数据集上，SIFT恢复了因朴素分解损失的准确率（最高27.6点），WSP的AUC达0.92，精确率0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的事实验证系统在标准基准上准确率高，但经常输出"支持"标签而其引用的证据实际上并不支持该主张。结构化分解是自然的检查方式，但刚性提取协议会丢失完整主张上下文的细粒度信息。

Method: 提出SIFT方法：对提取的证据跨度进行以完整主张为条件的重评分；同时引入WSP（Warranted Supports Proportion）指标，一个自动NLI检查，用于验证引用的推理是否蕴含该主张。在FEVER、SciFact、5PILS和DP四个数据集上使用四种开源骨干模型进行评估。

Result: SIFT恢复了因朴素分解损失的准确率（最高27.6个百分点），同时WSP高于直接提示方法；WSP与人工黄金证据校准的AUC为0.92，精确率为0.98。

Conclusion: SIFT和WSP能有效改善事实验证系统中证据与标签的逻辑一致性，实现高精度的自动评估。

Abstract: Fact-checking systems built on LLMs achieve high verdict accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet routinely output Supports labels whose cited evidence does not license the claim. Structured decomposition is the natural way to inspect those warrants, but rigid extraction protocols strip the full-claim context that facets need. We introduce SIFT -- claim-conditioned re-scoring of extracted evidence spans against the full claim -- paired with WSP (Warranted Supports Proportion), an automatic NLI check that the cited warrant entails the claim. We evaluate on FEVER, SciFact, 5PILS, and DP across four open-source backbones. SIFT recovers accuracy on cells where naive decomposition costs up to 27.6 points, while raising WSP above direct prompting; WSP itself calibrates against human gold evidence at AUC 0.92 and precision 0.98.

</details>


### [56] [Measuring User's Mental Models of Speech Translation in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24644)
*HyoJung Han,Nishant Balepur,Jordan Boyd-Graber,Marine Carpuat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过跨语言问答任务，探究用户对语音翻译系统的心智模型，发现用户通过实践、源语言知识和语音转写能更好地预测系统错误，从而提升人机协作效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数百万人每天使用机器翻译工具，但学界对用户如何感知系统能力与局限性的了解甚少。

Method: 提出基于跨语言问答的框架，用户在接受机器翻译输出或请求专业重译之间做出选择，通过分析用户行为与准确率趋势，考察其对系统错误位置的预测能力及心智模型的演化过程。

Result: 用户通过实践能建立更强的心智模型；源语言知识有助于提升模型；用户主要依赖表层错误线索；提供语音转写有助于心智模型的发展。

Conclusion: 跨语言问答任务作为研究机器翻译心智模型的下游任务具有前景，有助于加深对人机协作的理解。

Abstract: Millions of people use machine translation (MT) tools daily, yet little is known about their perception of what systems can and cannot do. This paper studies users' mental models of speech translation systems through a new framework based on cross-lingual question answering, where users either accept MT output or request professional re-translation to answer questions based on the information presented in a foreign language. By analyzing user behavior and accuracy trends across varying translation qualities, we examine to what extent they can predict where the system is likely to be wrong, and how this mental model evolves. Users develop stronger mental models with practice, especially when they have some knowledge of the source language, primarily by relying on surface-level error cues. Moreover, providing speech transcriptions can help users develop better mental models. Our results show the promise of cross-lingual question answering as a downstream task for studying MT mental models and advancing our understanding of human-AI collaboration.

</details>


### [57] [Harmonic: Hierarchical State Space Models for Efficient Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24650)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Harmonic是一种分层状态空间模型（SSM），通过堆叠三个不同时间尺度的递归层级，每层接收下层预测误差而非隐藏状态。在长序列任务上显著优于Transformer和Mamba，支持高达64K tokens的训练，且计算复杂度为O(L)。在1B参数规模下，替换TinyLlama的注意力层可消除RoPE位置编码的长度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer模型在长序列任务中面临O(L²)的计算复杂度瓶颈，而Mamba等SSM模型在大规模长序列上仍存在性能不足和内存限制问题。作者希望设计一种既能保持线性复杂度、又能有效处理超长序列（≥32K tokens）的语言模型架构。

Method: Harmonic采用三层递归层级结构，每层运行在不同时间尺度上。关键设计是：每层接收下层预测误差（prediction error）而非原始隐藏状态作为输入，这种残差式设计有助于层级间的信息传递和去相关。三个层级从快到慢逐步抽象，形成分层处理机制。

Result: 1）在enwiki8上，Harmonic在1K/8K/32K tokens下分别优于同等参数量Transformer（28M）1.4%/6.7%/11.4%，优于Mamba 0.7-1.8%；2）64K tokens时仅Harmonic（6.169 bpt）能在80GB H100上训练，Mamba和Transformer均OOM；3）WikiText-103上H-TF差距为1.7%-7.2%；4）1B参数Hallamonic模型在1K-8K序列上保持稳定loss，而TinyLlama在超过2K RoPE限制后性能崩溃（Lambada上8K时差距+9.4 bpt）。

Conclusion: Harmonic通过分层预测误差传递机制，实现了对超长序列的高效建模，在保持O(L)计算复杂度的同时，在多个长度上超越Transformer和Mamba。其消除位置编码长度限制的特性，使其成为大规模长上下文语言建模的可行替代方案。

Abstract: We present Harmonic, a hierarchical state space model (SSM) for language modeling. The architecture stacks three recurrent levels at progressively slower timescales; each level receives the prediction error of the level below as input, rather than its raw hidden state. On enwiki8 with equal token budgets, Harmonic outperforms a comparable Transformer (28M params) by +1.4% at 1K tokens, +6.7% at 8K tokens, and +11.4% at 32K tokens (bpt, lower is better). It also outperforms Mamba at every tested length by 0.7--1.8%. At 64K tokens, both Mamba and Transformer run out of memory on an 80GB H100; Harmonic trains successfully, reaching 6.169 bpt. Results replicate on WikiText-103 (H-TF gap +1.7% to +7.2% across 1K--32K). At 1B parameter scale, replacing all attention layers in TinyLlama 1.1B with HarmonicBlock eliminates the RoPE positional encoding limit: the resulting Hallamonic model maintains stable loss across sequence lengths 1K--8K on two independent clean benchmarks (Lambada and fineweb-edu held-out), while TinyLlama degrades catastrophically past its 2K-token RoPE limit (gap: +9.4 bpt at seq=8K on Lambada). Compute is O(L) per forward pass vs. O(L^2) for attention.
  Logs: https://github.com/Omibranch/harmonic-logs.

</details>


### [58] [AI-PAVE-Br: Leveraging Large Language Models for Enhanced Product Attribute Value Extraction through a Golden Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24655)
*Murilo Gazzola,Hugo Gobato Souto,Samuel Silva,Júlia Schubert Peixoto,Felipe Siqueira,André Luis Pedroso de Morais,Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对巴西电商领域葡萄牙语产品属性值提取（PAVE）的挑战，提出了基于大语言模型的AI-PAVEBr系统，并构建了首个高质量人工标注的葡萄牙语PAVE基准数据集Golden Set。实验证明，AI-PAVEBr通过提示工程显著优于传统命名实体识别方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西电商领域产品数据呈爆炸式增长且高度复杂，传统的产品属性值提取方法难以处理葡萄牙语的 linguistic nuances 和产品描述的多样性，亟需专门针对葡萄牙语的鲁棒且高效的PAVE系统。

Method: 1) 构建AI-PAVEBr系统，利用大语言模型结合针对性提示工程进行属性值提取；2) 创建Golden Set数据集，采用人工标注的方式，按实体、类别、子类别三层结构进行精细标注；3) 将AI-PAVEBr与传统命名实体识别基线方法进行对比实验。

Result: AI-PAVEBr在葡萄牙语电商产品属性值提取任务上，性能显著优于传统的命名实体识别基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文为巴西这一重要非英语市场提供了可扩展的高精度PAVE解决方案，并向NLP社区贡献了首个公开可用的葡萄牙语PAVE基准数据集Golden Set，为未来相关研究奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: The explosive growth and complexity of product data within the dynamic Brazilian e-commerce landscape demand robust and specialized methods for structured information extraction. Traditional approaches to Product Attribute Value Extraction (PAVE) often struggle with the linguistic nuances and sheer diversity of product descriptions in Portuguese. To address this critical gap, this paper introduces two major contributions. First, we present AI-PAVEBr, a specialized system engineered with Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform high-accuracy PAVE specifically for Brazilian e-commerce catalogs. Second, to facilitate reproducible research and provide a definitive benchmark, we introduce and share the Golden Set, a new, meticulously curated, and manually annotated dataset for PAVE in Portuguese. We detail the creation process and structure (Entity, Category, Subcategories) of this high-quality reference set. Our experiments conclusively show that AI-PAVE-Br, leveraging targeted prompt engineering, dramatically outperforms conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) baselines. This work not only delivers a superior, scalable solution for a major non-English market but also enriches the NLP community with a valuable, publicly available resource for future PAVE research.

</details>


### [59] [DREAM: Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24667)
*Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DREAM通过将检索器生成的查询-文档相似度分数注入冻结LLM的注意力头中，利用自回归下一token预测损失来训练稠密检索模型，无需成对标注数据，在BEIR和RTEB基准上取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索嵌入模型通常需要对比学习目标训练，这依赖于标注的正负文档对，成本高昂且难以获取。本文探索是否可以利用LLM的自回归下一token预测目标为稠密检索提供训练信号，从而减少对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 提出DREAM框架：将检索器输出的查询-文档相似度分数注入冻结LLM的选定注意力头中，控制每个候选文档在LLM预测目标输出时的注意力权重，利用下一token预测损失通过注意力机制反向传播梯度来训练检索器。

Result: 在BEIR和RTEB基准上，使用0.5B至3B参数的嵌入骨干网络，DREAM在不同模型规模下均一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: DREAM展示了通过自回归建模训练稠密检索器的有效途径，提供了一种无需人工标注对比对的检索模型训练方法。

Abstract: Dense retrieval embedding models are a fundamental component of modern retrieval-based AI systems. Most dense retrievers are trained with contrastive objectives, which require labeled positive and negative document pairs that are often costly and difficult to obtain. In this work, we investigate whether the autoregressive next-token prediction objective of a large language model (LLM) can provide supervision for dense retrieval. The intuition is simple: if a document contains information relevant to a query, conditioning on that document should make the target output easier for the LLM to predict. A key challenge is that the next-token prediction loss is computed inside the LLM, while the retriever is a separate embedding model. To address this challenge, we propose DREAM (Dense Retrieval Embeddings via Autoregressive Modeling), which injects retriever-generated query-document similarity scores into selected attention heads of a frozen LLM. During training, these scores determine how much attention each candidate document receives while the LLM predicts the target output. The resulting prediction loss provides gradients for retriever training through the attention mechanism. We evaluate DREAM on retrieval benchmarks BEIR and RTEB using embedding backbones ranging from 0.5B to 3B parameters. DREAM consistently outperforms existing baselines across different model scales. These results demonstrate that DREAM provides a promising approach for training dense retrievers through autoregressive modeling.

</details>


### [60] [CN-NewsTTS Bench: a target-level automatic benchmark for raw-input Chinese news TTS pronunciation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24714)
*Shijun Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1，一个面向中文新闻TTS产品的开放目标级基准测试，用于评估系统在无需用户干预下对密集书面形式的正确发音能力。最佳系统严格准确率为0.879，多个系统低于0.60。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文新闻文本包含大量密集书面形式（如分数、连字符模型名、范围、单位符号、百分比、英文缩写及中英拉混写名称），这些形式在真实听力工作流中频繁出现，但现有TTS系统常出现保留书写字符串但改变口语含义的问题。现有解决方案依赖用户侧规则、LLM重写、SSML提示或手动编辑，缺乏自动化评估基准。

Method: 构建包含200条开发集、800条公开测试集及992个可自动评估目标的基准数据集；采用三ASR集成模型生成固定转录文本；设计自动目标评分器；在七个产品级TTS系统上进行评估，并报告ASR路径诊断、ASR子集消融、类别级结果、置信区间及供应商配置元数据。

Result: 最佳系统严格准确率达到0.879，但多个系统低于0.60，表明当前中文新闻TTS产品在书面形式正确发音方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 现有产品级TTS系统对中文新闻中密集书面形式的发音准确性差异巨大，该基准可为中文TTS产品提供标准化评估方法，推动相关技术改进。

Abstract: Chinese news text contains dense written forms such as scores, hyphenated model names, ranges, unit symbols, percentages, English abbreviations, and mixed Chinese-Latin-digit names. These forms are frequent in real listening workflows, and a text-to-speech (TTS) system can preserve the written string while changing the spoken meaning. We introduce CN-NewsTTS Bench v0.1, an open target-level benchmark for evaluating whether Chinese news TTS products pronounce such targets correctly from raw text, without user-side rules, LLM rewriting, SSML hints, or manual edits. The release contains a 200-record development set, an 800-record public test set, 992 public auto-evaluable targets, fixed transcripts from a three-ASR ensemble, an automatic target scorer, and initial results for seven product TTS systems. We additionally report ASR-route diagnostics, ASR-subset ablations, category-level results, confidence intervals, and provider configuration metadata. The best system reaches 0.879 strict accuracy, while several systems remain below 0.60.

</details>


### [61] [Task Decomposition for Efficient Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24734)
*Nupoor Gandhi,Emma Strubell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将结构化标注任务分解为子任务，以降低聚合推理负荷，并基于中心理论的形式化模型指导分解与分配，从而在预算约束下提升标注质量与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化标注的高质量注释成本高昂，人工标注劳动密集，模型标注虽廉价但需大量验证；同时，不同标注者（人类或模型）在领域和语言专长上存在异质性，如何在此场景下合理分配标注任务尚不明确。

Method: 将标注任务分解为子任务以减少推理负荷；受中心理论启发，基于有效标注空间的自由度建立推理负荷形式化模型；通过识别中心（即由子任务实现的显著锚点实体）约束输出空间复杂度；提供分解指南及子任务分配流程。

Result: 证明识别中心能约束输出空间复杂度；分解并优先推进中心识别可降低聚合推理负荷；通过先前工作中的示例展示了改进的成本效率；提出在固定预算下最大化质量的子任务分配流程。

Conclusion: 将复杂结构化标注任务分解为子任务，并基于中心理论指导分解与异构标注者间的分配，是一种提升标注质量与成本效率的有效途径。

Abstract: High-quality annotations of structured representations are expensive to collect over large corpora. Manual annotation of structure is laborious, and model-based annotation, although cheaper to generate, requires expensive validation and potentially significant supervision to ensure that the annotation quality is strong enough to be useful downstream. In traditional annotation workflows, annotation of each complete example is performed end-to-end by a single annotator. However, structured annotation is complex, and each aspect of the task represents a unique challenge with an associated inferential load for a given annotator. Modern annotation projects can incorporate heterogeneous groups of annotators, including both models and human annotators with varying domain and linguistic expertise. It remains unclear, however, how to redesign annotation tasks in this setting, where efforts are discriminately allocated across heterogeneous annotators with respect to distinct annotation challenges.
  We propose to decompose annotation tasks into sub-tasks in order to reduce the aggregate inferential load of annotation projects. Inspired by the notion of centers from centering theory, we introduce a formal model of inferential load based on the degrees of freedom in the space of valid annotations. Using this model, we show that identifying these centers (i.e. salient anchor entities realized by annotation sub-tasks) constrains the output space complexity, and decompositions which isolate and advance center identification reduce the aggregate inferential load. We provide guidelines for decomposing complex structured annotation tasks, supported by examples demonstrating improved cost-efficiency from our prior work. Finally, we present a procedure for allocating sub-tasks across annotators to maximize quality under a fixed budget.

</details>


### [62] [CANDLE: Character-level Arabic Noise Deduplication using Lightweight Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24758)
*Faris Alasmary,Taif Nono,Orjuwan Zaafarani,Kholood Al Tabash,Ahmad Ghannam,Anas Salamah,Shouq Sadah,Lahouari Ghouti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CANDLE 提出了一种基于 CTC 的轻量级阿拉伯语字符级噪声去重系统，无需人工规则或词典，在三个基准上显著优于分类基线，并通过知识蒸馏实现 3 倍深度压缩，同时将 LLM 分词器 Fertility 降低 12.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本中重复字符可能表示正确拼写或非正式字符延长（如社交媒体），现有方法依赖手工规则、词典或形态分析器，缺乏通用且轻量的解决方案。

Method: 将字符去重建模为序列对齐问题，首次引入 Connectionist Temporal Classification（CTC）处理该任务，使用基于字符的编码器进行归一化；并采用知识蒸馏将 6 层 CTC 模型压缩为 2 层学生模型。

Result: 在干净新闻、人工标注歧义案例和真实社交媒体文本三个基准上，CTC 模型 Sentence Error Rate（SER）低至 5.37%，大幅优于分类基线；蒸馏后深度减少 3 倍，性能损失极小；去重后阿拉伯语 LLM 分词器 Fertility 相对降低 12.8%。

Conclusion: CANDLE 证明了 CTC 用于字符级去重的有效性，轻量且无需语言资源，能直接降低 LLM 推理开销并提升上下文窗口利用率，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Handling repeated characters in text can be tricky, since they can represent either the correct spelling of a word or informal character elongation often seen in social media posts. We present CANDLE, a lightweight system for character-level Arabic noise deduplication that addresses this challenge without relying on handcrafted rules, dictionaries, or morphological analyzers. At the heart of CANDLE is a novel application of Connectionist Temporal Classification (CTC) to this task, a formulation not previously explored for character deduplication, which frames normalization as a sequence alignment problem over a character-based encoder. Evaluated on three benchmarks spanning clean newspaper, manually curated ambiguous cases, and real-world social media text, the CTC model achieves a Sentence Error Rate (SER) as low as $5.37\%$ and consistently outperforms a classification-based baseline by a large margin. To reduce inference overhead, we distill the 6-layer CTC model into a 2-layer student, achieving a $3\times$ depth reduction with minimal performance degradation. Beyond deduplication accuracy, normalization yields a practical downstream benefit: a relative reduction in tokenizer fertility of up to $12.8\%$ across a diverse set of Arabic LLM tokenizers, directly lowering inference costs and improving context window utilization. We release all code and models publicly to support reproducibility and advance future research\footnote{https://github.com/abjadai/candle}.

</details>


### [63] [Posterior Refinement: Fast Language Generation via Any-Order Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24773)
*Manan Agarwal,Sheel Shah,Chanhyuk Lee,Jaehoon Yoo,Jerry Huang,Seunghoon Hong,Aditi Raghunathan,Jinwoo Kim,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FMLM+框架通过为流映射语言模型引入掩蔽式噪声调度和后验精炼策略，在保持单步生成效率的同时实现自适应自校正，以32倍更少的NFE达到离散基线性能，弥合了掩蔽扩散模型与流映射语言模型之间的速度-质量权衡差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非自回归生成模型中，掩蔽扩散模型存在分解误差，同时生成多个token时样本质量下降；流映射语言模型虽然实现了高质量的少步生成，但牺牲了推理时的灵活性。两者均未能充分发挥非自回归范式在迭代精炼中的潜力。

Method: 提出FMLM+框架，在FMLM基础上引入掩蔽式噪声调度，使模型在单步生成完整序列的同时能够后验地评分每个token的全局一致性；并基于此提出后验精炼策略，实现推理时的自适应自我校正。

Result: FMLM+结合后验精炼在多种基准上以32倍更少的NFE达到离散基线的性能水平，在速度-质量权衡上优于MDM和FMLM两类模型家族。

Conclusion: FMLM+为高保真语言建模提供了可扩展的基础，有效弥合了MDM的灵活性与FMLM的高效性之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Non-autoregressive generation offers a powerful paradigm for iterative refinement, allowing models to recursively critique, erase and regenerate arbitrary subsets of tokens. However, existing non-autoregressive models fail to realize this potential. Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) suffer from factorization error, causing sample quality to collapse when generating multiple tokens simultaneously. Flow Map Language Models (FMLMs) circumvent this bottleneck via joint sequence transport for excellent few-step generation, but sacrifice the inference-time flexibility of MDMs. We introduce FMLM+, a framework that bridges this gap by equipping FMLM with masking-style noise schedules. While generating the full sequence in a single step, FMLM+ simultaneously scores the global consistency of each token a posteriori. We leverage this to introduce Posterior Refinement, a novel inference-time refinement strategy that enables the model to adaptively self-correct its outputs, matching the performance of discrete baselines with 32x fewer NFEs. Across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that FMLM+ with Posterior Refinement improves the speed--quality tradeoff over both MDM and FMLM families, providing a scalable foundation for high-fidelity language modeling.

</details>


### [64] [Are We Ready For An Agent-Native Memory System?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24775)
*Wei Zhou,Xuanhe Zhou,Shaokun Han,Hongming Xu,Guoliang Li,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从数据管理角度对LLM智能体记忆系统进行了系统性实验研究，将记忆分解为表示存储、提取、检索路由、维护四大模块，评估了12个代表性系统和2个基线，发现没有单一架构适用于所有场景，并揭示了成本-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估将智能体记忆视为黑盒，仅通过端到端任务指标衡量，忽略了操作成本、模块间架构权衡、动态知识更新下的鲁棒性等系统级问题。

Method: 提出分析框架将智能体记忆分解为四个核心模块（记忆表示与存储、提取、检索与路由、维护），在5个基准工作负载（涵盖11个数据集）上评估12个代表性记忆系统和2个基线，进行端到端评估和细粒度消融研究。

Result: 1) 没有单一架构在所有场景占优，有效性取决于记忆结构与工作负载瓶颈的匹配度；2) 细粒度消融实验量化了各模块对表示保真度、检索精度、更新正确性、长期稳定性的影响；3) 局部维护比全局重组更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 指向构建真正面向智能体的原生记忆系统的有前景方向。

Abstract: Memory for large language model (LLM) agents has rapidly evolved from simple retrieval-augmented mechanisms into a data management system that supports persistent information storage, retrieval, update, consolidation, and dynamic lifecycle governance throughout agent execution. Despite this evolution, existing evaluations still benchmark agent memory mainly through end-to-end task success metrics (e.g., F1, BLEU), while treating the underlying system as a monolithic black box. As a result, critical system-level concerns, including operational costs, architectural trade-offs across memory modules, and robustness under dynamic knowledge updates, remain insufficiently explored. In this paper, we present a systematic experimental study of agent memory from a data management perspective. We propose an analytical framework that decomposes agent memory into four core modules: memory representation and storage, extraction, retrieval and routing, and maintenance. Under this framework, we evaluate 12 representative memory systems and two reference baselines across five benchmark workloads spanning 11 datasets. Our extensive end-to-end evaluation shows that no single architecture dominates across all scenarios; instead, effectiveness depends heavily on how well the memory structure aligns with the workload bottleneck. Furthermore, through fine-grained ablation studies, we quantify their individual effects on representation fidelity, retrieval precision, update correctness, and long-horizon stability. Finally, we reveal cost-performance trade-offs under realistic workloads, showing localized maintenance is more cost-efficient than global reorganization. Based on these findings, we identify promising directions towards building truly agent-native memory systems. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/OpenDataBox/MemoryData.

</details>


### [65] [Paying to Know: Micro-Transaction Markets for Verified Product Information in Agentic E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24783)
*Filippos Ventirozos,Matthew Shardlow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将电商购物从推荐匹配模式转变为微型交易信息市场，由自主代理通过微支付逐步解锁商品验证信息，并指出NLP领域应关注成本最优信息获取、数据定价、实体解析等问题而非对话流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商将购物聊天机器人视为推荐或转化工具，其目标是匹配用户与商品并促成交易。但随着自主代理的出现，瓶颈已从商品匹配转向获取关于商品的可靠、决策相关信息。

Method: 提出基于微支付轨道的自主代理电商架构，采用免费增值模式，买家代理通过逐步付费解锁卖家与评论者提供的信息（如服务记录、第三方检测报告等），评论者信任度通过声誉机制评估。

Result: 该架构奖励真正的产品质量，产生比基于排名的商店更真实的竞争。

Conclusion: 将愿景转化为具体的NLP问题：成本最优信息获取、数据定价与谈判、实时实体解析、基于价值交换的落地、隐私保护的人格建模，强调这些问题比对话流畅度更值得领域关注。

Abstract: Commercial NLP treats the shopping chatbot as a recommender or a conversion tool: its job is to match a user to a catalogue entry and close a sale. We argue that the arrival of agent-native micro-payment rails (e.g., x402, AP2) changes what is scarce. When the buyer is an autonomous agent that can investigate exhaustively, the bottleneck is no longer matching products but acquiring trustworthy, decision-relevant information about them. We envision agentic e-commerce as a micro-transaction market for verified information: buyer agents spend fractions of a cent to progressively unlock seller- and reviewer-supplied data -- service histories, third-party test reports, bills of materials, audited sales and support metrics -- paid for a la carte under a freemium model, with reviewer trust scored reputationally. We sketch the architecture of such a market and argue that it rewards genuine product quality and yields truer competition than ranking-based storefronts. We then translate the vision into concrete NLP problems -- cost-optimal information acquisition, data pricing and negotiation, real-time entity resolution, grounded value exchange, and privacy-preserving persona modelling -- and argue that these, not chat fluency, deserve the field's attention.

</details>


### [66] [SHERLOC: Structured Diagnostic Localization for Code Repair Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24820)
*Hovhannes Tamoyan,Sean Narenthiran,Erik Arakelyan,Mira Mezini,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHERLOC是一个无需训练的LLM框架，通过结构化假设驱动推理实现代码库故障定位，在SWE-Bench上达到SOTA性能（84.33% accuracy@1），并将定位结果注入修复代理后可提升5.95个百分点的解决率，同时减少36.7%的定位token和23.1%的总token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在仓库级编码任务中约一半预算用于故障定位，而专用定位框架仅被评估为文件检索而非可操作的诊断，无法提供修复代理所需的诊断上下文。

Method: SHERLOC是一个训练无关框架，将推理型LLM与紧凑的仓库工具和自恢复机制配对，采用结构化假设驱动的探索与推理，无需微调或多智能体编排。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Lite上达到84.33% accuracy@1，在SWE-Bench Verified上达到81.27% recall@1；约30B参数的模型匹配或超越其他代理方法；注入修复代理后解决率平均提升5.95个百分点，定位token减少36.7%，总token减少23.1%。

Conclusion: SHERLOC证明了在不进行微调或多智能体协调的情况下，通过结构化推理和紧凑工具结合，可以显著提升代码故障定位的准确性和效率，并为下游修复任务提供可操作的诊断信息。

Abstract: LLM agents solve repository-level coding tasks through multi-turn tool use, but utilize half their budget on locating faults before editing. Dedicated localization frameworks have emerged, yet are still evaluated as file retrieval rather than actionable diagnosis, producing locations without the diagnostic context a repair agent needs. We introduce SHERLOC (Structured Hypothesis-driven Exploration and Reasoning for Localization), a training-free framework pairing a reasoning LLM with compact repository tools and self-recovery, without fine-tuning or multi-agent orchestration. SHERLOC reaches state-of-the-art localization across model scales: 84.33% accuracy@1 on SWE-Bench Lite and 81.27% recall@1 on SWE-Bench Verified; at ~30B parameters, it matches or outperforms other agentic methods. Injecting our locations and diagnostic findings into repair agents yields, on average, +5.95 pp resolve rate on SWE-Bench Verified while cutting localization and total tokens by 36.7% and 23.1%.

</details>


### [67] [L3Cube-MahaPOS: A Marathi Part-of-Speech Tagging Dataset and BERT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24825)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个大规模马拉地语标准POS标注数据集L3Cube-MahaPOS（32,354句），并系统评测了六类模型，最佳系统（MahaBERT-v2）取得88.67%准确率和81.67%宏F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马拉地语虽有超过8300万使用人口，但在标注语料库和标准化评估基准方面严重匮乏，且其丰富的形态、相对自由的语序、缺乏大写约定以及与印地语、英语的混杂代码为计算建模带来独特挑战。

Method: 构建了32,354句新闻文本人工标注数据集，采用16标签Universal Dependencies对齐方案；设计了含Unicode标准化、天城文感知分词和噪声过滤的预处理流程；在HMM、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM+CharCNN、MuRIL和MahaBERT-v2六类模型上进行基准测试。

Result: 最佳系统（MahaBERT-v2）在15个评估标签上达到88.67%的token级准确率和81.67%的宏F1分数。

Conclusion: 发布了数据集、标注指南和训练好的模型检查点，以促进马拉地语NLP领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Part-of-Speech (POS) tagging is a foundational NLP task underpinning machine translation, information extraction, and syntactic parsing. Despite Marathi being spoken by over 83 million people and ranking among the top twenty most spoken languages worldwide, it remains severely under-resourced in annotated corpora and standardised evaluation benchmarks. Marathi presents unique challenges for computational modelling owing to its rich morphology, relatively free word order, lack of capitalisation conventions, and pervasive code-mixing with Hindi and English. We introduce L3Cube-MahaPOS, a gold-standard POS tagging dataset for Marathi comprising 32,354 manually annotated sentences drawn from news text. Annotation was performed entirely manually by a team of Marathi-proficient annotators following a 16-tag Universal Dependencies-aligned scheme. A structured preprocessing pipeline covering Unicode normalisation, Devanagari-aware tokenisation, and noise filtering ensures label consistency across all splits. We benchmark the dataset across six model families spanning HMM, CRF, BiLSTM, BiLSTM+CharCNN, MuRIL, and the Marathi-specific transformer MahaBERT-v2. The best system achieves 88.67\% token-level accuracy and a macro-F1 of 81.67% over 15 evaluated tag classes. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and trained model checkpoints to foster further research in Marathi NLP.

</details>


### [68] [Less is More: Quality-Aware Training Data Selection for Scientific Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24828)
*Maria Nefeli Paraskevopoulou,Tatiana Passali,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建并发布了包含188万篇PMC文章的生物医学长文本摘要数据集，分析了作者撰写摘要的参考质量，发现质量感知的数据选择能提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学文献摘要数据集存在两个问题：一是作者撰写的摘要作为参考摘要的质量和与原文的对齐程度参差不齐；二是公开可用的科学摘要数据集在规模和结构上对现代长上下文模型仍然有限。

Method: 构建了包含188万篇PMC文章的大规模生物医学摘要数据集；使用基于源文档和基于模型的质量指标分析作者撰写摘要的参考质量；利用质量信号指导训练数据选择。

Result: 作者撰写的摘要与全文的对齐程度存在差异；在匹配训练规模下，基于高质量子集训练的模型优于随机采样，并在事实性指标上可达到或超越更大的随机子集。

Conclusion: 参考质量是科学摘要的重要因素，质量感知的数据选择可以提高训练效率。

Abstract: Scientific long-document summarization datasets commonly treat author-written abstracts as gold reference summaries, although their quality and alignment with the source article vary. At the same time, publicly available scientific summarization datasets remain limited in scale and structure for modern long-context models. In this work, we address both challenges by a) constructing and releasing one of the largest biomedical and life science datasets for long-document summarization, containing 1.88 million PMC articles, and b) analyzing the reference quality of author-written abstracts with source-grounded and model-based metrics. We show that author-written abstracts vary in their alignment with the full article and that these quality signals can guide training-data selection. Training on selected high-quality subsets outperforms random sampling at matched training sizes and can match or exceed larger random subsets on factuality-oriented metrics. Our findings suggest that reference quality is an important factor in scientific summarization and that quality-aware data selection can improve training efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [69] [Cryptographic certificates of validity for trustworthy AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23768)
*Murdoch J. Gabbay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向Agentic AI系统的密码学有效性证书方法，通过将策略条件编译为多项式约束的见证校验问题，并利用简洁的密码学证明系统（可选零知识）来认证条件满足性，在形式化验证与密码学认证之间建立中间地带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难困境：形式化验证虽强但难以扩展到复杂AI系统，且需要信任编译器/验证器；而纯密码学认证虽可验证执行迹但缺乏形式化保证。需要一种方法既能提供可独立验证的数学保证，又不需要验证者信任Agent或重新执行计算。

Method: 将正确性/策略条件形式化为逻辑谓词，编译为多项式约束的见证校验问题，利用简洁的密码学证明系统（如SNARKs）生成可独立验证的证书，支持零知识选项。

Result: 给出了核心数学翻译，将方法与证明携带代码、zkVM、形式化方法和Agent治理相关联，识别了规范、审计和部署方面的关键实施问题。

Conclusion: 该方法为Agentic AI系统提供了一种介于形式化验证与密码学认证之间的实用中间方案，使得Agent的行为可以附带可独立验证的策略合规证明，无需信任Agent或重新执行计算。

Abstract: We propose cryptographic certificates of validity for agentic AI systems. The core idea is to formally specify a correctness or policy condition as a logical predicate, compile this predicate to a witness-checking problem over polynomial constraints, and use a succinct cryptographic proof system (and optionally zero-knowledge) to certify that the condition holds.
  This offers a middle ground between formal verification of source code, and cryptographic authentication. An agent's action can be accompanied by an independently checkable proof that it satisfies an agreed formal policy, without requiring the verifier to trust the agent or to re-execute computation. We outline the approach at a high level, give the core mathematical translation, relate the proposal to proof-carrying code, zkVMs, formal methods, and agent governance, and note the specification, auditing, and deployment questions that a full implementation must answer.

</details>


### [70] [AutoPRAC: Automating Attack Discovery for PRAC-Based Rowhammer Defenses using Model Checkers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23905)
*Joyce Qu,Gururaj Saileshwar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoPRAC，首个利用模型检查器自动化测试PRAC（DDR5按行激活计数）防御安全性的方法。通过将PRAC实现建模为有界状态机并检查安全属性，AutoPRAC自动发现了MOAT防御中的一个新漏洞——其计数器重置策略允许超过Rowhammer阈值34次激活未被检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PRAC设计的安全性评估依赖人工构造的攻击模式，缺乏形式化验证其安全属性的方法，也缺少自动化检测实现缺陷的技术。

Method: AutoPRAC将PRAC实现建模为有界状态机，针对最坏情况的预言机攻击者检查安全关键的安全性属性。若属性被违反，框架会生成具体的反例轨迹（对应成功攻击）。

Result: 发现了MOAT（最先进的PRAC防御）中一个先前未报告的漏洞：其计数器重置策略允许最多34次激活未被检测到，超过Rowhammer阈值。

Conclusion: AutoPRAC能够自动发现Rowhammer缓解措施中的细微安全缺陷，可作为PRAC设计早期阶段攻击发现的设计辅助工具。

Abstract: Per-Row Activation Counting (PRAC) in DDR5 is a specification to mitigate Rowhammer attacks by tracking activations per row and triggering mitigative refreshes when needed. However, the security of PRAC designs is currently evaluated using human-crafted attack patterns and we lack formal verification of their security properties, or automated techniques to detect implementation flaws. In this work, we present AutoPRAC, the first automated technique to test the security of PRAC-based defenses using model checkers. AutoPRAC models PRAC implementations as bounded state machines and checks security-critical safety properties against a worst-case oracle attacker. If a property is violated, the framework produces a concrete counterexample trace corresponding to a successful attack. Using AutoPRAC, we uncover a previously unreported flaw in MOAT, a state-of-the-art PRAC defense, in its counter-reset policy that allows up to 34 activations to go undetected above the Rowhammer threshold. Our results demonstrate that AutoPRAC can automatically discover subtle security flaws in Rowhammer mitigations and serves as an early-stage design aid for attack discovery on PRAC designs.

</details>


### [71] [BipBipCache: Pipeline-Aware Integration of Low-Latency Tweakable Encryption in an Embedded Cache Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23941)
*Corbin Hibler,Firas Hassan,Eric McKanna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BipBipCache，一个集成BipBip可调分组密码的直接映射缓存控制器，通过C³风格的24+40位分解对缓存数据和标签进行实时加密，并将6周期加密延迟优化为3周期有效写入代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级和嵌入式处理器将敏感数据存储在片上SRAM缓存中，这些数据在断电或物理探测后仍可读取，除非密文本身保存在存储阵列中。现有方案难以同时满足强安全性与低延迟要求。

Method: 设计BipBipCache直接映射缓存控制器：1) 集成BipBip可调分组密码(TBC)，采用C³风格的24+40位字分解实现实时加密；2) 重构首个流水线硬件BipBip加密器（原规范面向解密器），并与3周期解密器协调嵌入缓存数据通路；3) 加密器前三个流水级与标签解密和命中检测重叠，实现有效写入代价优化。

Result: 6周期加密延迟经流水线重叠优化后，实际写入提交仅需3周期（命中验证后）。硬件验证通过官方BipBip C++参考（各5个测试向量）；FPGA资源报告：Xilinx Artix-7上3,356个LUT（占16.1%），其中密码逻辑约占79%的LUT。

Conclusion: BipBipCache有效解决了SRAM缓存数据的机密性问题，抵御冷启动、总线探测和SRAM读出攻击，且通过流水线重叠设计显著降低了加密延迟的实际影响，并在FPGA上完成了端到端硬件验证。

Abstract: Consumer and embedded processors store sensitive data in on-chip SRAM caches that remain readable after power loss or physical probing unless ciphertext is maintained in the memory array itself. This paper presents BipBipCache, a direct-mapped cache controller that integrates the BipBip tweakable block cipher (TBC) to encrypt cache data and tags in real time using a C$^3$-style 24+40 bit decomposition of each 64-bit word. We reconstruct the first pipelined hardware BipBip encryptor from a decryptor-centric specification and coordinate it with a 3-cycle decryptor inside the cache datapath.
  Our threat model targets confidentiality of cache-resident contents against cold-boot, bus, and SRAM readout attacks. A key architectural result is that 6-cycle encryption latency does not fully translate into 6-cycle write penalty: the first three encryptor stages overlap with tag decryption and hit detection, leaving an effective 3-cycle write commitment after hit verification. We verify encryptor and decryptor correctness against the official BipBip C++ reference (five vectors each), report FPGA resource utilization on Xilinx Artix-7 (3,356 LUTs, 16.1% of device; crypto logic ~79% of LUTs), and confirm end-to-end operation on hardware.

</details>


### [72] [Maestro Order: A Model-Agnostic Orchestration Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23983)
*Hidayet Aksu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Maestro Order的模型无关编排框架，通过分解、集成、验证和递归四种结构原语，配合预算感知控制器，将不可靠的求解器（如大语言模型）转化为可靠的问题求解系统。仿真表明验证可几何级数提升可靠性，预算控制器能以极小成本达到目标可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型等基础模型单次前向传播速度快但不可靠（产生幻觉），虽然正确率足够有用但错误率也足够危险。现有方法缺乏系统性的、模型无关的、预算可控的可靠性提升框架。

Method: 提出Maestro Order编排框架：(1) 四种结构原语：分解(decompose)、集成(ensemble)、验证(verify)、递归(recurse)；(2) 预算感知控制器，按边际可靠性/成本比分配验证和投票计算资源；(3) 将模型封装为统一接口的黑盒求解器，在线测量验证器的判别能力；(4) 确定性、可观测、容错的工程实现。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真定量验证了理论预测：验证可将可靠性从0.55几何级数提升至0.98（两级）和0.999（四级）；投票仅在正确率超过随机水平时有效且受限于共享错误；预算感知控制器能以投票方式极小部分成本达到目标可靠性。

Conclusion: 核心建议：构建鲁棒的验证器（而非更好的求解器）、多样化求解器、让控制器将计算分配到信息量最大的环节。指出了验证器欺骗、错误相关性、分解误差累积等失败模式。

Abstract: A single forward pass of a capable model is a fast, fluent, and unreliable problem-solver: it is right often enough to be useful and wrong often enough to be dangerous; in language models, such confident errors are known as hallucinations. We present Maestro Order, a model-agnostic orchestration harness that turns unreliable solvers into reliable problem-solving systems by composing them according to four structural primitives (decompose, ensemble, verify, and recurse) and a budget-aware controller that decides where to spend compute. The harness treats any model as a black-box base solver behind a uniform interface, layers a verifier ensemble whose discrimination is measured online, and allocates verification and voting to the stages with the highest marginal reliability per unit cost. We give the architecture, the message and state schema, the controller algorithm, and the engineering that makes it deterministic, observable, and fault-tolerant. We then specify an evaluation methodology (reliability at fixed cost, coverage, calibration, and ablations) and report results from a faithful Monte Carlo simulation of the harness over a parameterized solver/verifier model. The simulation reproduces the predicted laws quantitatively: verification amplifies reliability geometrically (e.g. $0.55\to0.98$ with two gates, $\to0.999$ with four), voting helps only above chance and is limited by shared errors, and a budget-aware controller reaches a target reliability at a small fraction of the cost of voting alone by selecting the cheapest mechanism for each regime. We close with failure modes (verifier gaming, correlated errors, and decomposition error compounding) and concrete guidance: build robust checkers, diversify solvers, and let the controller put compute where the information is.

</details>


### [73] [PixJail: Self-Evolving Paper-to-Pipeline Reproduction for Text-to-Image Jailbreak Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24081)
*Leyi Sheng,Han Sun,Zhen Sun,Yuntao Yue,Jinlin Wu,Xinlei He,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PixJail 是一个自进化的论文到流水线智能体框架，用于可复现的文本到图像（T2I）越狱评估。它能够根据论文和参考代码自动构建攻击模块与评估流水线，并通过记忆库实现经验复用，在11种方法上以平均2.1%的误差复现了原始结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 T2I 越狱技术发展迅速，但基准测试和复现工作流难以跟上节奏。更重要的是，T2I 越狱评估不是单次提示级别的测试，而是涉及提示转换、图像生成、安全过滤和多模态判断等多个阶段的流水线级问题，导致不同论文的结果难以可靠复现和公平比较。

Method: 提出 PixJail 框架，包含：(1) 论文特定的攻击模块和可运行评估流水线的自动构建；(2) 统一合约确保复现一致性；(3) 记忆库存储论文摘要、攻击演化模式、可复用模板、失败案例和版本化工件，支持未来复现复用先前经验。

Result: 复现了11种代表性T2I越狱方法（包括有代码和无代码论文），在原始设置下以平均2.1%、中位数0%的误差准确恢复了先前结果。

Conclusion: PixJail 可以作为未来 T2I 越狱复现和评估的统一基础，显著减少人工工作量。
