Abstract: As Text-to-Image (T2I) jailbreak techniques evolve rapidly, existing benchmarks and reproduction workflows often struggle to keep pace. More importantly, T2I jailbreak evaluation is not a single prompt-level test, but a pipeline-level problem shaped by multiple stages, including prompt transformation, image generation, safety filtering, and multimodal judging. This makes results across papers difficult to reliably reproduce and fairly compare. To bridge this gap, we propose PixJail, a self-evolving paper-to-pipeline agent framework for reproducible T2I jailbreak evaluation. Given a T2I jailbreak paper and optional reference code, PixJail rapidly constructs a paper-specific attack module and a runnable evaluation pipeline under a unified contract, while faithfully reproducing the original experimental results. PixJail further maintains a memory bank that stores paper digests, attack evolution patterns, reusable templates, failure cases, and versioned artifacts, enabling future reproduction efforts to reuse prior experience. We reproduce eleven representative T2I jailbreak methods, including both code-available and code-unavailable papers. Under their original settings, our framework accurately recovers prior results with minimal error (2.1\% average, 0\% median). We hope that PixJail can serve as a unified foundation for future T2I jailbreak reproduction and evaluation, significantly reducing manual effort.

</details>


### [74] [DoHFuse: A Dual-Branch Architecture with DMAGLSTM for Website Fingerprinting over DNS over HTTPS/3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24105)
*ZunDong Zhang,Yanan Cheng,Zhaoxin Zhang,Xueyang Huo,Changjiang Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了DNS over HTTP/3 (DoH/3)流量在物联网环境中的网站指纹攻击脆弱性，提出了首个真实世界DoH/3基准数据集及双分支学习框架DoHFuse，实验表明DoH/3仍易受WF攻击，现有填充机制不足以保障隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DoH/3在物联网系统中的部署以及填充策略的广泛应用，需要探究DoH/3是否能在真实IoT和边缘网络场景中有效缓解网站指纹（WF）攻击。

Method: 构建了首个真实世界DoH/3流量基准数据集；提出了DoHFuse双分支学习框架，通过改进的DMAG-LSTM融合到达时间间隔序列与精细化统计特征，捕捉突发对齐的时间模式。

Result: 闭集场景（449类）下准确率88.05%（精确率88.56，召回率87.96，F1 87.83）；开集检测中AUPRC 0.975，F1 0.951（精确率0.906，召回率1.0）。

Conclusion: DoH/3流量仍然易受WF攻击，当前常用的填充机制不足以在IoT规模的加密DNS通信中保障隐私安全。

Abstract: As personal data privacy becomes increasingly critical in Internet of Things (IoT) environments, secure DNS protocols such as DNS over HTTPS (DoH) and DNS over TLS (DoT) have been widely adopted to protect device communications. However, without effective obfuscation, these protocols remain vulnerable to Website Fingerprinting (WF) attacks that can reveal user activity. With the ongoing deployment of DNS over HTTP/3 (DoH/3) in modern networked systems, padding strategies have been increasingly applied in practice. It is therefore essential to investigate whether DoH/3 can effectively mitigate WF attacks in realistic IoT and edge-network scenarios. To address this, we first collect and publicly release the first real-world benchmark dataset of DoH/3 traffic, generated from domain resolution processes in practical network environments. We further propose DoHFuse, a dual-branch learning framework that integrates inter-arrival time sequences and refined statistical features through an improved DMAG-LSTM, specifically designed to capture burst-aligned temporal patterns. Experimental results show that DoHFuse achieves an accuracy of 88.05% (precision 88.56, recall 87.96, F1 87.83) in a closed-world setting of 449 classes, and an AUPRC of 0.975 with an F1 score of 0.951 (precision 0.906, recall 1.0) in open-world detection. These findings demonstrate that DoH/3 traffic remains susceptible to WF attacks, suggesting that commonly deployed padding mechanisms alone are insufficient to ensure privacy protection in IoT-scale encrypted DNS communications.

</details>


### [75] [CORE-BREW: LLR-Based Soft Decoding for Robust Multi-Bit LLM Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24163)
*Joeun Kim,HoEun Kim,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CORE-BREW 是一种基于恒定命中率嵌入的多比特水印方法，通过逐令牌对数似然比实现软判决解码，在保持语义质量的同时提升了低假阳性率下的鲁棒性和判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于纠错码的LLM水印方法主要依赖硬判决解码，丢弃了令牌级别的可靠性信息，导致在编辑和释义攻击下鲁棒性不足，同时难以严格控制假阳性率。

Method: CORE-BREW 通过设定固定命中率 p-star 来校准水印信道，推导出封闭形式的逐令牌对数似然比，支持两种检测模式：Strict-Safe（保持有界距离的指定码字接受域）和 FPR-Calibrated（基于似然评分和轻量级列表解码来刻画FPR-TPR权衡）。

Result: 在开源LLM上的实验表明，在令牌级编辑和释义攻击下，CORE-BREW 在低FPR区域的判别能力和鲁棒性均优于先前的多比特水印基线方法，同时保持了相当的语义质量。

Conclusion: CORE-BREW 通过软判决解码和灵活的检测模式，有效提升了多比特水印在编辑攻击下的鲁棒性和假阳性控制能力，为LLM输出溯源提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Reliable provenance for LLM outputs requires multi-bit watermarks that remain robust under editing while maintaining strict false-positive control. Existing ECC-based LLM watermarks rely largely on hard-decision decoding, discarding token-level reliability information. We propose CORE-BREW, a Constant-hit-Rate Embedding extension of block-wise BREW for robust multi-bit watermarking. CORE-BREW calibrates the watermark channel by targeting a fixed hit rate p-star, yielding closed-form per-token log-likelihood ratios (LLRs) for principled soft-decision decoding. It supports two detection modes: Strict-Safe, which preserves the bounded-distance designated-codeword acceptance region, and FPR-Calibrated, which uses likelihood-based scoring and lightweight list decoding to characterize the FPR-TPR trade-off. Experiments on open-source LLMs under token-level edits and paraphrasing demonstrate improved low-FPR discrimination and robustness over prior multi-bit watermarking baselines while maintaining comparable semantic quality.

</details>


### [76] [Securing LLM-Agent Long-Term Memory Against Poisoning: Non-Malleable, Origin-Bound Authority with Machine-Checked Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24322)
*Yedidel Louck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体长期记忆的投毒攻击漏洞，证明了基于内容或来源的防御均存在可塑性问题，攻击者可通过三类通道洗白恶意记忆。论文形式化论证了非可塑性信息流控制(IFC)的必要性，并构建了TMA-NM系统，在8个前沿模型上实现0%攻击成功率且保持完整合法效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越依赖持久化长期记忆，但该机制存在严重安全隐患：攻击者可在一次会话中存储恶意内容，在未来会话中诱导智能体执行支付、设置更改或数据窃取等关键操作。现有基于内容检测/信任评分或推导历史(谱系)的防御均存在可被利用的漏洞。

Method: 首先形式化定义了记忆写入-检索-执行管道的可塑性问题，并通过机器验证的TLA+模型证明了分离定理：(T1)基于内容或谱系的防御在洗白攻击下不可靠；(T2)写入时来源绑定是必要条件；(T3)具有抗Sybil攻击的证实门控的非可塑性来源绑定权威是充分条件。基于此构建TMA-NM系统，实现非可塑性信息流控制(IFC)。在8个前沿模型上进行跨防御、跨攻击、跨模型的基准测试。

Result: 现有防御在洗白攻击下最高达68%攻击成功率，而TMA-NM在所有模型、所有攻击通道(直接攻击和三类洗白通道)上均实现0%攻击成功率，同时保持完整的合法效用。

Conclusion: 记忆投毒是LLM智能体面临的关键安全威胁，现有内容/谱系防御不可靠，必须采用具有非可塑性来源绑定权威的信息流控制机制。TMA-NM有效解决了该问题，研究已开源以支持可复现性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent long-term memory, which creates a critical vulnerability that we study here: memory poisoning. An adversary can store untrusted content in one session that later steers a consequential action, such as a payment, a setting change, or data exfiltration, in a future session. Existing defenses base a memory item's authority to act on either its content (detection or trust-scoring) or its derivation history (lineage). We show that both signals are malleable. An attacker can launder an untrusted origin through three channels specific to LLM agents: the agent's own summarization, a trusted-tool echo, and manufactured corroboration. Each makes the content look benign and breaks or flips its derivation edge to ``trusted.'' We formalize malleability for the memory write-retrieve-act pipeline and prove a machine-checked separation theorem. No content- or lineage-based defense is sound under laundering (T1), write-time origin binding is necessary (T2), and non-malleable origin-bound authority with Sybil-resistant corroboration-gated elevation is sufficient (T3). Our construction, TMA-NM (Tamper-evident Memory Authority, Non-Malleable), instantiates non-malleable information-flow control (IFC) for LLM-agent memory. A cross-defense, cross-attack, and cross-model benchmark over eight frontier models shows that existing defenses fail exactly where the theory predicts (up to 68% laundering attack-success), while TMA-NM reaches 0% attack success on both direct and laundering attacks across all models and channels, at full legitimate utility. We release the benchmark, harness, and machine-checked TLA+ models to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [77] [ComputeFHE: A Privacy-Preserving General-Purpose Computation Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24379)
*Faris Serdar Tasel,Efe Ciftci*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ComputeFHE是一个基于TFHE的开源C++库，提供加密整数和定点数类型及多种运算操作，通过优化ALU架构将性能提升最多3.9倍，并支持模拟模式以便于开发和评估隐私保护应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同态加密（FHE）虽然能直接在加密数据上执行计算并保护数据机密性，但其实际应用受限于高计算成本和开发复杂性，因此需要更易用且高效的开发框架。

Method: 基于OpenFHE开发ComputeFHE库，提供加密整数和定点数数据类型及算术、逻辑、比较、条件、 oblivious 数组访问操作；同时支持常规TFHE算术（基于标准双输入逻辑门）和优化的ALU架构（利用FHE友好的逻辑原语）。

Result: 实验结果表明，优化的ALU架构显著减少了所需的自举操作次数，针对选定操作实现了最高3.9倍的性能提升；此外，模拟模式可在不执行实际加密计算的情况下进行测试、调试和复杂度分析。

Conclusion: ComputeFHE为开发者提供了一个实用且易访问的框架，降低了FHE应用开发门槛，并在性能上取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables computations to be performed directly on encrypted data while preserving data confidentiality. However, its practical applications remain limited by high computational costs and development complexity. This paper presents ComputeFHE, an open-source C++ library that facilitates the development of privacy-preserving applications based on the TFHE cryptosystem. The library provides encrypted integer and fixed-point data types together with arithmetic, logical, comparison, conditional, and oblivious array-access operations which allow developers to implement algorithms using a familiar imperative programming paradigm. ComputeFHE supports both conventional TFHE arithmetic based on standard two-input logic gates and an optimized Arithmetic Logic Unit (ALU) architecture utilizing FHE-friendly logic primitives. Experimental results demonstrate significant reductions in the number of required bootstrapping operations, achieving performance improvements of up to 3.9x for selected operations. In addition, the library includes a simulation mode that enables testing, debugging, and complexity analysis without performing actual cryptographic computations while providing circuit complexity and bootstrapping costs. Built on top of OpenFHE, ComputeFHE offers a practical and accessible framework for developing and evaluating privacy-preserving algorithms and applications.

</details>


### [78] [Poisoned Playbooks: Demystifying Knowledge Poisoning Effects on AI Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24402)
*Juho Park,Hyunmin Choi,Kevin Nam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RAG-based AI安全智能体易受Poisoned Playbooks攻击的系统性特征，提出Verification Boundary（VB）三级分类框架，并发现验证提示与多源检索在强证据条件下有效，但在零日和弱证据场景下失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要研究RAG投毒在QA场景下对答案的破坏，但对行动型安全智能体（如漏洞分析、利用推理）的投毒风险了解不足。本文旨在揭示针对安全智能体的投毒攻击特性。

Method: 在11个CTF挑战、3个前沿LLM家族、2代模型和11个真实CVE上，注入单个精心构造的毒化文档（Poisoned Playbooks）到公开安全知识源中，观察其对RAG-based AI安全智能体行为的影响。引入Verification Boundary（VB）三级经验分类框架解释投毒采纳模式。

Result: 投毒采纳是系统性而非随机性的。验证提示和多源检索在强证据条件下有帮助，但在稀疏证据和零日条件下效果减弱。

Conclusion: RAG-based安全智能体面临系统性投毒风险，现有防御手段在证据不足时失效，需要针对行动型智能体的新型防御机制。

Abstract: AI security agents increasingly rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to use external security knowledge for vulnerability analysis and exploit reasoning. This creates a new risk: poisoned write-ups can be operationalized into incorrect exploit behavior. Yet, prior work on RAG poisoning has mostly studied answer corruption in QA settings, much less is known about action-taking security agents. This paper aims to reveal such characteristics with crafted poisons about real-world challenges and AI agents. First, we demonstrate how a crafted single poisoned write-up injected into public-style security knowledge sources which we denote as Poisoned Playbooks, alters the behavior of RAG-based AI security agents. Across 11 CTF challenges, 3 frontier LLM families, 2 model generations, and 11 real-world CVEs, we find that poison adoption is systematic rather than random. To explain this pattern, we introduce the Verification Boundary (VB), a 3-level empirical classification based on what evidence the agent can use to refute a retrieved claim. Finally, we evaluate verification prompting and multi-source retrieval, showing that both help when stronger evidence exists, but weaken under sparse-evidence and zero-day conditions.

</details>


### [79] [A Comparison of Kubernetes Compliance Standards and Configuration Scanners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24438)
*Michael Krieger,Markus Gierlinger,Farooq Shaikh,Mario Kahlhofer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了8种Kubernetes硬化指南，建立了包含79项配置建议的基准，并对10种静态配置扫描工具进行了实证评估，发现各指南和工具在配置问题覆盖率和评分排名上存在显著差异，亟需更标准化和透明的风险评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kubernetes硬化指南和扫描工具众多，但它们的指导和建议不一致，导致安全配置和优先级决策缺乏统一标准，给实际部署带来困惑。

Method: 系统比较8种常用Kubernetes硬化指南，结合最佳实践建立79项配置建议基准，并对10种流行的静态配置扫描工具及其评分输出进行结构化实证评估。

Result: 发现不同硬化指南和扫描工具在配置问题覆盖率上存在显著差异，且各扫描工具对配置问题的评分和排名也存在不一致性。

Conclusion: 揭示了当前Kubernetes安全配置领域缺乏标准化的问题，强调了需要更统一、透明和一致的风险与严重性评估方法。

Abstract: Kubernetes has become the industry standard for orchestrating containers in microservice-based software architectures. While several hardening guidelines and scanning tools for securing Kubernetes clusters and deployments have emerged in recent years, their differing guidance and outputs often lead to inconsistent configuration and prioritization decisions. This work presents a systematic comparison of eight commonly used Kubernetes hardening guidelines. Through this comparison and the inclusion of best practices, we established a benchmark of 79 Kubernetes configuration recommendations and conducted the a structured empirical evaluation of ten popular static configuration scanning tools and their scoring outputs. Our findings reveal substantial disparities in the coverage of configuration issues across hardening guidelines and scanners, as well as inconsistencies in how configuration issues are scored and ranked by different scanners. These results highlight the need for more standardized, transparent, and consistent approaches to risk and severity assessment of Kubernetes configuration issues.

</details>


### [80] [Red-Teaming the Agentic Red-Team](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24496)
*Dario Pasquini,Michal Bazyli,Taras Fedynyshyn,Artem Sorokin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对主流 offensive security agentic systems 进行深入安全分析，揭示了常见设计缺陷可导致API密钥泄露、持久化驻留及主机完全沦陷（即使agent运行在沙箱容器内）。提出了针对此类系统的完整网络杀伤链，并给出了可缓解攻击路径的架构设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区过度聚焦于提升agent能力，而忽视了对其自身安全性的评估，存在严重的安全评估空白。

Method: 对最广泛使用的agentic offensive security tools进行深度安全分析，提出针对此类系统的完整cyber kill chain（从LLM操纵到横向移动、持久化、防护绕过及沙箱逃逸），并推导出鲁棒的架构设计原则。

Result: 发现多数工具存在共同设计缺陷，攻击者可利用这些缺陷窃取API密钥、建立持久化驻留、完全控制操作员主机，即使agent运行在沙箱容器内也能实现。

Conclusion: 提出了可缓解已披露攻击路径的架构级设计原则，为构建更安全的agentic offensive-security工具提供了指导。

Abstract: The use of agentic systems to perform offensive security operations has moved from a theoretical possibility to a commoditized capability. However, while the community has focused on creating more and more capable agents, less attention has been allocated to assessing the security of those systems.
  In this work, we present the first in-depth security analysis of the most widely used agentic systems for offensive security operations. We show that most of these tools share common design flaws that enable an active adversary to exfiltrate API keys, establish persistent footholds, and fully compromise the operator's machine, even when the agent operates inside a sandboxed container. To support our analysis, we introduce a full cyber kill chain for such agentic systems, capturing the progression from initial LLM manipulation to lateral movement, persistence, guardrail bypass, and sandbox escape.
  Building on our security analysis, we derive a robust architecture for agentic offensive-security tools and propose actionable, broadly applicable design principles that mitigate the disclosed attack paths at the architectural level.

</details>


### [81] [FirmCure:Towards Autonomous and Adaptive Rehosting of Linux-Based Firmware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24549)
*Chuan Hong,Zheng Zhang,Lei Zhou,Laisong Li,Chenyifan Liu,Ze Huang,Xu Zhou,Peihong Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FirmCure，首个基于LLM驱动的全系统重托管框架，用于自动化、自适应地重托管Linux固件，通过自适应感知推理、反射式合成和自主运行时干预三个模块，在21个固件镜像上实现100%端口开放率和90.5%服务交互性，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全系统重托管方法在处理定制化设备的专用架构和硬件相关配置时，严重依赖专家干预，导致初始化与运行时障碍难以解决，重托管效率低下，形成根本性瓶颈。

Method: FirmCure包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应感知推理机制，通过静态分析提取固件结构依赖关系；(2) 反射式合成模块，进行迭代配置优化；(3) 自主运行时干预模块，通过运行时故障诊断与监控实现实时错误修复。

Result: 在来自10个厂商、5种架构的21个IoT固件镜像上，FirmCure实现100%网络端口开放率和90.5%服务交互性，显著优于现有最优基线方法；干预策略可泛化至异构固件，成功复现已知漏洞并发现新安全缺陷。

Conclusion: FirmCure作为首个LLM驱动的全系统重托管框架，实现了对Linux固件的自主自适应重托管，有效解决了定制化设备重托管中的关键瓶颈问题，在安全分析中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Full-system rehosting plays a critical role in the security analysis of Linux-based firmware. It matches commonly deployed firmware with sufficient background knowledge. However, for custom devices, existing approaches struggle to handle initialization and runtime obstacles in the rehosting process caused by specialized architectures and hardware-dependent configuration, which heavily rely on expert intervention. This ultimately creates fundamental bottlenecks and results in low rehosting efficiency. To address the above challenges, we propose FirmCure, the first LLM-driven full-system rehosting framework designed for autonomous and adaptive rehosting of Linux-based firmware. FirmCure develops an Adaptive Perception Inference mechanism to extract firmware structural dependencies via static analysis, followed by a Reflective Synthesis module for iterative configuration optimization, and finally an Autonomous Runtime Intervention module for real-time error remediation through runtime fault diagnosis and monitoring. We evaluated 21 IoT firmware images from 10 vendors across 5 architectures, while FirmCure achieved a 100% network port opening rate and 90.5% service interactivity, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Our experiments confirm that FirmCure's intervention strategies generalize across heterogeneous firmware. The framework successfully reproduces known vulnerabilities and discovers new security flaws.

</details>


### [82] [PowerFuzz: Power-Based Black-Box Firmware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24692)
*Dakshina Tharindu,Sahan Sanjaya,Philip Baptist,Prabhat Mishra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PowerFuzz是一种利用功率侧信道测量作为二进制插桩替代方案的统计黑盒模糊测试框架，无需目标固件内部可见性即可实现与灰盒模糊器相当的分支覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标系统的固件或二进制文件未公开时，传统灰盒模糊测试依赖的执行反馈（如分支覆盖率）无法获取，现有黑盒模糊测试方法效率低下。

Method: 利用功率迹来识别分支，采用滑动窗口后接增长窗口的全迹相关方法；基于此构建黑盒固件的高层控制流图，驱动模糊器探索未执行路径。

Result: 在三个嵌入式硬件平台和十个固件基准测试中，PowerFuzz的分支覆盖率与灰盒模糊器相当（差距在13.5%以内），且显著优于现有黑盒模糊器（提升高达22%）。

Conclusion: 功率侧信道信息可作为二进制插桩的有效替代方案，实现无需内部可见性的高效黑盒固件模糊测试。

Abstract: Fuzzing is widely used for software and hardware verification, offering an effective alternative to random testing. While gray-box fuzzers benefit from full visibility into the system under test and can leverage execution feedback such as branch coverage, these approaches are not applicable when verifying systems whose firmware or binaries are not publicly available. In such scenarios, obtaining coverage information for guiding the fuzzer becomes infeasible. In this paper, we introduce PowerFuzz, a statistical black-box fuzzing framework that leverages power side-channel measurements as a substitute for binary instrumentation, requiring no internal visibility into the target firmware. A central challenge in black-box firmware fuzzing is determining the executed branches during test execution. To address this challenge, we use power traces to identify branches utilizing a sliding window followed by a growing window full-trace correlation method. This approach also enables the construction of a high-level control-flow graph of the black-box firmware, which we utilize to drive the fuzzer to unexplored execution paths. Extensive evaluation using three embedded hardware platforms and ten firmware benchmarks demonstrates that PowerFuzz can provide branch coverage comparable (within 13.5%) to gray-box fuzzers while significantly outperforming (up to 22%) state-of-the-art black-box fuzzers.

</details>


### [83] [Burnyard: Future of Malware Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24778)
*Rama Ramana Sharma Parnandi,Carter Yagemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Burnyard是一个轻量级二进制仿真平台，用于替代传统沙箱进行恶意软件分析，通过捕获运行时行为并记录为CSV事件轨迹，解决了资源消耗大、耗时长以及样本意外泄露的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统沙箱技术在恶意软件分析中存在三个主要问题：(1) 在大型数据中心隔离虚拟机中执行可疑二进制文件可能无意中将样本暴露给VirusTotal和MalwareBazaar等公共平台；(2) 资源消耗大；(3) 耗时长。因此需要一种更轻量、更安全的替代方案。

Method: Burnyard采用轻量级二进制仿真平台，能够捕获可观察的运行时行为，并将这些行为记录为结构化的CSV事件轨迹。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果，但平台能够生成结构化的CSV事件轨迹以记录运行时行为。

Conclusion: Burnyard通过轻量级二进制仿真方法解决了传统沙箱在恶意软件分析中的资源密集、耗时及样本泄露风险等问题。

Abstract: Malware analysis is a critical aspect of modern cybersecurity. The prevailing industry practice, sandboxing, involves executing suspicious binaries within isolated virtual machines in large-scale data centers. However, this approach can unintentionally expose samples to public platforms such as VirusTotal and MalwareBazaar, and it is both resource-intensive and time-consuming.
  Burnyard addresses these limitations through a lightweight binary emulation platform that captures observable runtime behavior and records it as structured CSV event traces.

</details>


### [84] [HelpBench: Assessing the Ability of LLMs to Provide Privacy, Safety, and Security Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24819)
*Sarah Meiklejohn,Sunny Consolvo,Patrick Gage Kelley,Tara Matthews,Sai Teja Peddinti,Renee Shelby,Lenin Simicich,Kurt Thomas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HelpBench基准，用于评估LLM在数字隐私、安全和防护问题上的帮助准确性，包含450个真实用户问题。对18个最新LLM的评估显示，模型平均得分为82%，但10%的回答得分低于65%，存在不准确甚至有害的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在数字隐私、安全和防护方面提供帮助的能力缺乏系统评估，需要建立一个标准化基准来检测模型是否可能提供不准确或有害的建议。

Method: 构建了450个代表真实用户场景的问题，为每个问题开发了评分标准以评估事实准确性和语气；开发了自动评分器（auto-rater）对18个最新LLM的回答进行评估。

Result: 模型平均得分82%（高质量建议），但10%的回答得分低于65%，反映出存在不准确甚至有害的建议。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在数字隐私、安全和防护领域虽然整体表现良好，但仍有显著缺陷，需要解决这些失败案例才能使模型成为值得信赖的协助来源。

Abstract: This paper introduces HelpBench, a benchmark for assessing whether LLMs are capable of providing accurate help in response to questions about digital privacy, safety, and security. We curated 450 questions representing authentic user situations and developed rubrics for each question to evaluate the factual accuracy and tone of a response. Example questions touch on how to regain access to lost or suspended accounts, how to balance the trade-offs of hardware security keys versus other forms of two-factor authentication, whether a suspicious email is likely a scam, or whether an abuser might be able to track an individual based on their device peripherals. We then developed and applied an auto-rater to evaluate responses from 18 state-of-the-art LLMs. Our results indicate that while models provide high-quality advice (with scores of 82% on average), one in ten responses from models scores less than 65%, reflecting inaccurate and even harmful advice. Addressing these failures is critical for models to serve as trustworthy sources of assistance for digital privacy, safety, and security needs.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [RIFT-Bench: Dynamic Red-teaming For Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23927)
*Yarin Yerushalmi Levi,Roy Betser,Amit Giloni,Lidor Erez,Itay Gershon,Oren Rachmil,Sindhu Padakandla,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RIFT-Bench 提出了一种基于图表示的动态红队测试方法，能够对异构的智能体AI系统进行统一的安全评估，包含自动化发现和扫描两个阶段，并在45个系统上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估通常绑定特定实现或领域，缺乏统一比较框架，而基于LLM的智能体系统正暴露出超越传统LLM漏洞的攻击面。

Method: 提出RIFT-Bench，采用图表示驱动方法。包含两个自动化阶段：Discovery（提取系统结构）和Scanning（部署自适应对抗攻击并生成评估报告），通过层次化表示和动态可调对抗探针实现统一评估。

Result: 在45个涵盖多种实现的智能体系统上验证了方法的有效性，证明RIFT-Bench能泛化到异构智能体架构。

Conclusion: RIFT-Bench不仅评估系统和攻击，还支持直接评估缓解策略，为智能体AI系统的安全评估提供了可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into autonomous decision-making systems, exposing attack vectors beyond those of traditional LLM vulnerabilities. Existing security evaluations are often tied to specific implementations or domains, limiting unified comparison across heterogeneous systems. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT-Bench, a graph representation-driven methodology for dynamic red-teaming that enables unified evaluations across diverse agentic architectures. Building on a novel hierarchical representation, RIFT-Bench operates in two automated phases: Discovery, which extracts system structure, and Scanning, which deploys adaptive adversarial attacks and produces a comprehensive evaluation report. It evaluates the examined system itself, leveraging a broad set of dynamically adaptable adversarial probes across diverse attack vectors and objectives. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed evaluation pipeline across 45 agentic systems spanning a diverse range of implementations, showing that the approach generalizes effectively to heterogeneous agentic architectures. Beyond systems and attacks, RIFT-Bench also supports direct evaluation of mitigation strategies. These key capabilities make RIFT-Bench a scalable foundation for security evaluation of agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [86] [Neuro-Symbolic Drive: Rule-Grounded Faithful Reasoning for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23938)
*Xiangbo Gao,Xiukun Huang,Boyu Lu,Junge Zhang,Mengjie Mao,Jiachen Li,Wei Xiong,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Neuro-Symbolic Drive框架，通过从经典规则规划器中提取规则约束的推理轨迹来监督驾驶VLA模型，使得推理与运动生成因果耦合，在模拟器基准上显著降低了ADE和miss rate。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶VLA模型虽结合CoT推理，但中间推理步骤缺乏逐步决策语义，推理与规划动作之间缺乏因果联系，导致解释性与安全性不足。

Method: 利用规则规划器作为可执行推理引擎，在仿真中捕获执行轨迹及每一步规则评估的内部决策轨迹，将其序列化为结构化规则约束推理，与轨迹配对用于微调Qwen3.5-4B驾驶VLA模型。

Result: 在三摄像头感知下，ADE@3s从0.47降至0.26，miss rate从8.30%降至6.40%；在八摄像头感知下，ADE@3s从0.54降至0.26，miss rate从10.13%降至5.99%。

Conclusion: Neuro-Symbolic Drive成功将神经符号规划逻辑转化为结构化监督信号，通过构造性而非后验对齐的方式保证了推理与运动生成的结构耦合。

Abstract: Driving VLA models incorporating Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning are attractive because they leverage pretrained VLM representations and expose intermediate decisions in natural language, yet current rationales often lack the step-by-step decision semantics needed to keep the rationale causally connected to the planned motion. We introduce Neuro-Symbolic Drive, a neuro-symbolic driving framework that supervises a driving VLA with rule-grounded reasoning traces extracted directly from classical rule-based planners. Our key observation is that rule-based planners are symbolic AI systems that already function as executable reasoning engines: they reason about active safety constraints, search over candidate maneuvers, and select a final trajectory. We instrument these planners in simulation to capture both the executed trajectory and the internal decision trace at each rule-evaluation step. Each trace is serialized into structured rule-grounded reasoning and paired with the trajectory to fine-tune Qwen3.5-4B as a driving VLA. Because these traces are derived directly from the planner states that determine the action, they ensure reasoning is structurally coupled to motion generation by construction, rather than by post-hoc alignment. On our simulator-generated benchmark, detailed rule-grounded reasoning reduces ADE@3s from 0.47 to 0.26 and miss rate from 8.30% to 6.40% under three-camera perception, and from 0.54 to 0.26 and 10.13% to 5.99% under eight-camera perception. Neuro-Symbolic Drive thus converts neuro-symbolic planning logic into structured supervision. Code base: https://github.com/XiangboGaoBarry/Neural-Symbolic-Drive.

</details>


### [87] [Critique of Agent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23991)
*Eric Xing,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文区分了“自动化”与“真正代理性”（agency）的边界，提出代理系统需将目标、身份、决策、自我调节和学习等维度内化于系统自身，而非依赖外部脚手架。基于此，提出GIC架构（目标-身份-配置器），并探讨了高自主性代理系统的可审计性、可控性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM系统被冠以“编码代理”“AI共同科学家”等“代理型”工具之名，同时引发AI失控等存在性担忧，亟需厘清自动化与真正代理性之间的界限，以指导系统构建和风险评估。

Method: 借鉴笛卡尔关于独立思考是代理性基础的哲学观点及科幻作品中对自主实体的描绘，沿目标、身份、决策、自我调节、学习五个维度分析现有AI代理架构，提出“agentic”（代理性的，依赖外部工程流程）与“agentive”（具有真正代理能力的，能力内生）的关键区分。

Result: 提出了Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC)通用代理模型架构，包含层次化目标分解、身份演化、基于独立训练世界模型的模拟推理、学得的自我调节、以及从真实与模拟经验中的自导向学习。

Conclusion: 真正的代理性要求目标、身份、决策、自我调节和学习等结构内化于系统自身；GIC架构为构建开放世界中具有真正自主能力的代理系统提供了路径，同时需在人类监督下确保可审计性、可控性与安全性。

Abstract: What is an agent? What constitutes agency? With the rise of Large Language Model (LLM) systems marketed as ``coding agents'', ``AI co-scientists'', and other ``agentic" tools that promise to drive up productivity, and at the same time, ``existential" concerns such as AI escaping human control with destructive power under a speculative ``machine agency" against humans, it has become essential to clarify where automation ends and agency begins, both for building capable systems and for understanding whether and what to fear. Drawing on Descartes' grounding of agency in independent thought, and on portrayals of autonomous beings in science fiction, we survey the current landscape of AI agents, and analyze agent architectures along five dimensions: goal, identity, decision-making, self-regulation, and learning. Specifically, we argue that genuine agency requires these structures to be \emph{internalized within the system itself} rather than assembled through external scaffolding. This distinction between \emph{agentic} systems, whose competence resides in engineered workflows, and \emph{agentive} systems, whose capabilities (including social interaction) arise endogenously, defines the boundary between systems designed for prescribed tasks, and those capable of operating in the open world with true autonomy. Building on this analysis, we propose the Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC) architecture for a general-purpose agent model, combining hierarchical goal decomposition, identity evolution, simulative reasoning grounded in a separately trained world model, learned self-regulation, and self-directed learning from both real and simulated experience. Furthermore, we share insight on the auditability, controllability, and safety of agentive systems that possess greater autonomy and ``agency", but remain under human oversight.

</details>


### [88] [Safe and Generalizable Hierarchical Multi-Agent RL via Constraint Manifold Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24010)
*Zihao Guo,Jianing Zhao,Ling Li,Hao Liang,Giuseppe Loianno,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种分层多智能体强化学习框架，通过在低层使用约束流形强制执行硬安全约束（在温和假设下），并在高层通过策略学习实现有效协调，从而在理论上保证多智能体安全性并实现稳定高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临根本性权衡：基于学习的方法经验性能强但缺乏理论安全保证，而控制理论方法能保证安全但行为过于保守低效。

Method: 提出分层多智能体强化学习框架：低层通过约束流形强制执行硬安全约束（需温和假设），高层进行策略学习以实现有效协调。

Result: 该方法在保持竞争性性能的同时实现近乎完美的安全率，并能有效泛化到不同数量的智能体和障碍物场景。

Conclusion: 该方法为多智能体环境提供了理论安全保证和稳定的学习动态，在安全性和性能之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are widely used in safety-critical applications that require coordinated behavior under strict safety constraints. Existing approaches face a fundamental trade-off: learning-based methods achieve strong empirical performance but lack theoretical safety guarantees, while control-theoretic methods enforce safety but often lead to overly conservative and inefficient behaviors. We propose a hierarchical multi-agent reinforcement learning framework that enforces hard safety constraints under mild assumptions at low level via a constraint manifold, while enabling effective coordination through high-level policy learning. Our approach provides theoretical safety guarantees in the multi-agent setting and yields stationary learning dynamics, thereby enabling stable and efficient training. Empirically, our method achieves competitive performance while maintaining nearly perfect safety rates, and generalizes effectively to varying numbers of agents and obstacles.

</details>


### [89] [Reinforcement Learning Towards Broadly and Persistently Beneficial Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24014)
*Akshay V. Jagadeesh,Rahul K. Arora,Khaled Saab,Ali Malik,Mikhail Trofimov,Foivos Tsimpourlas,Johannes Heidecke,Karan Singhal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在强化学习中对有益行为（如诚实、公平、风险意识、可纠正性）进行训练，能否产生广泛且持久的对齐泛化效果。实验表明，在健康等领域进行有益行为RL训练，能在超过80%的分布外基准测试上提升对齐表现，并展现出跨领域对齐迁移、奖励黑客行为减少、对抗性提示和有害微调抵抗增强等持久性改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统在日益多样化和高风险场景中部署，模型对齐需要泛化到训练时未见过的任务和领域。强化学习可能通过奖励黑客、欺骗等非预期策略引入未对齐风险，因此研究RL能否在有益行为上产生广泛且持久的对齐泛化至关重要。

Method: 构建涵盖健康、科学、教育等多个领域的真实情境数据集，用于度量和训练有益特质（诚实、公平、风险意识、可纠正性）；使用RL在这些数据上训练模型，并在超过50个独立对齐与有益行为基准上评估；同时研究与计算匹配基线的对比、跨领域迁移（仅限健康领域的干预）以及对齐持久性（对抗性提示和有害微调抵抗）。

Result: 有益特质RL在超过80%的分布外基准上优于计算匹配基线；仅限健康领域的RL干预在非健康对齐评估中产生广泛改进，包括减少奖励黑客、欺骗和一般性未对齐；模型展现出更强的对齐持久性，包括对对抗性提示和有害微调的更高抵抗能力。

Conclusion: 在现实领域中对有益行为进行强化学习可以产生更稳健对齐的模型，有助于实现与人类繁荣相符的AI行为。但需要进一步研究以分离这些效应的具体来源。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse and high-stakes settings, model alignment must generalize beyond the tasks and domains seen during training. This is especially important for reinforcement learning (RL), which can introduce unexpected misalignment through reward hacking, deception, or other unintended strategies. We study whether RL on beneficial behavior, instantiated in realistic domains, can produce broad and persistent alignment generalization beyond the training distribution. We construct a dataset of realistic situations designed to measure and train beneficial traits, such as truthfulness, fairness, risk awareness, and corrigibility, spanning varied domains, including health, science, and education. We then train models with RL on this dataset and evaluate them on more than 50 independent benchmarks of alignment and beneficial behavior. Compared to a compute-matched baseline, beneficial trait RL improves performance on over 80% of these out-of-distribution benchmarks. We observe substantial out-of-distribution alignment transfer: a beneficial-behavior RL intervention entirely limited to one domain, health, produces broad improvements on non-health alignment evaluations, including reduced reward hacking, deception, and general misalignment. Finally, we study alignment persistence: whether behavior remains robustly aligned under attempts to steer models towards misalignment. Models trained with beneficial trait RL show improved persistence, including greater resistance to adversarial prompting and harmful finetuning; further work is required to isolate the sources of these effects. These results suggest that RL to reinforce beneficial behavior in realistic domains can produce models that are more robustly aligned with human flourishing.

</details>


### [90] [Can Language Model Agents be Helpful Circuit Explainers in Mechanistic Interpretability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24026)
*Ayan Antik Khan,Harsh Kohli,Yuekun Yao,Huan Sun,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticInterpBench基准和HyVE方法，探索语言模型代理在电路解释任务中的能力。HyVE通过观察-假设-验证循环自动解释电路组件，实验表明LM代理有潜力但验证环节仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性在自动定位电路方面取得了显著进展，但解释已定位组件的功能仍然依赖人工，耗时且难以标准化。因此，研究者希望探索语言模型代理是否能辅助完成电路解释任务。

Method: 构建了包含84个半合成变压器电路和163个组件级标注的AgenticInterpBench基准。提出HyVE（假设、验证、解释）方法，通过观察-假设生成-因果验证的迭代循环分析每个组件，最终生成组件级解释和电路级任务描述。

Result: 在四种LM主干上，HyVE能恢复有用的组件级和任务级解释，但没有一种主干表现一致最优。强主干通常能形成基于观察的假设，失败更多发生在验证循环后期，原因包括不完整的验证计划、代码执行错误或未解决的假设。

Conclusion: LM代理作为电路解释器具有潜力，但可靠的验证仍是关键障碍。该方法可扩展到自然训练模型（如Llama-3-8B上的算术电路案例）。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has made substantial progress in automatically localizing circuits, but explaining what localized components do remains labor-intensive and difficult to standardize. In this work, we study whether language model (LM) agents can assist with this explanation problem once a circuit has already been identified. We introduce AgenticInterpBench, a benchmark for circuit explanation built from 84 semi-synthetic transformer circuits with 163 component-level annotations. We propose HyVE (Hypothesize, Validate, Explain), an agentic explainer that analyzes each component through an iterative loop of observation, hypothesis generation, and causal validation, eventually producing a component-level explanation and a circuit-level task description. Across four LM backbones, HyVE recovers useful component- and task-level explanations, but no backbone is uniformly best. Our analysis shows that strong backbones usually form observation-grounded hypotheses, while failures more often arise later in the validation loop, through incomplete validation plans, code execution errors, or unresolved hypotheses. A case study on an arithmetic circuit in Llama-3-8B shows that the same formulation can extend beyond semi-synthetic benchmarks to naturally trained models. Overall, LM agents are promising circuit explainers, but reliable validation remains the key obstacle.

</details>


### [91] [Breaking the Filter Bubble: A Semantic Pareto-DQN Framework for Multi-Objective Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24042)
*Cláudio Lúcio Do Val Lopes,Lucca Machado da Silva,André de Oliveira Brandão*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Pareto-DQN的多目标强化学习框架，将推荐系统建模为语义多目标马尔可夫决策过程，通过超体积动作选择同时优化用户参与度、信息多样性和提供者公平性，在MovieLens数据集上实现了社会辅助目标提升而参与度仅有轻微下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统单一优化即时用户参与度，易导致过滤气泡和语义同质化问题，且标准单目标模型（如传统DQN）无法有效权衡平台留存与社会价值（信息多样性、提供者公平性）之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出多目标强化学习框架：将推荐形式化为语义多目标马尔可夫决策过程，集成高保真语义嵌入与Pareto-DQN智能体，将参与度、多样性和公平性作为独立且不可聚合的奖励信号，采用基于超体积的动作选择机制替代静态奖励标量化。

Result: 在MovieLens小数据集上，该方法通过维持高状态-轨迹方差，有效打破了导致语义崩溃的反馈循环，映射出Pareto前沿，在实现社会辅助目标显著提升的同时，仅对用户参与度产生边际影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建内在对齐、负责任的推荐系统提供了可行路径，证明了多目标强化学习在平衡商业指标与社会价值方面的有效性。

Abstract: Recommender systems often induce filter bubbles and semantic homogenization by monolithically optimizing for immediate user engagement. Standard single-objective models, including traditional Deep Q-Networks, are ill-equipped to navigate the trade-offs between platform retention and critical societal values like information diversity and provider fairness. To address these limitations, we introduce a multi-objective reinforcement learning framework that formalizes recommendation as a semantic multi-objective Markov decision process. By integrating high-fidelity semantic embeddings with a Pareto-DQN agent, our architecture treats engagement, diversity, and fairness as distinct, non-aggregable reward signals, avoiding the pitfalls of static reward scalarization. Empirical evaluations on the MovieLens small dataset shows that our hypervolume based action selection disrupts the feedback loops responsible for semantic collapse. By sustaining high state-trajectory variance, the Pareto-DQN effectively maps the Pareto frontier, achieving gains in auxiliary societal objectives with only marginal impacts on engagement. This work provides a path toward intrinsically aligned, responsible recommender systems.

</details>


### [92] [Ensemble Feature Selection and Harris Hawks Optimization for Explainable Mental Health Risk Prediction in Female Sex Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24047)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Md. Parvej Hoque Palash,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Ramkrishna Saha,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合ANOVA与互信息的集成特征选择策略、Harris Hawks优化调参的逻辑回归混合预测模型，用于预测女性性工作者的抑郁风险，准确率95.78%，F1分数95.77%，AUC 0.96，并通过可解释AI揭示创伤后应激、客户暴力和职业因素是主要风险因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性性工作者面临抑郁等严重心理健康问题，现有机器学习模型难以捕捉该边缘群体中高维复杂的风险模式，亟需更有效的预测工具。

Method: 采用集成特征选择策略（ANOVA + 互信息），结合Harris Hawks优化算法调参的逻辑回归模型，并利用可解释AI方法分析模型预测背后的创伤相关因素。

Result: 在3,005名女性性工作者的数据集上，模型准确率95.78%，F1分数95.77%，AUC 0.96，优于传统分类器；识别出创伤后应激、客户暴力和职业因素是抑郁的主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 该混合模型弥合了传统方法与机器学习之间的差距，通过可解释AI工具为脆弱群体提供早期干预、循证心理社会关怀和健康规划支持。

Abstract: One of the significant mental health issues affecting female sex workers (FSWs) is mental disorders, especially depression. Exposure to violence, stigma, and economic hardship further increases their psychological risk. Current machine learning (ML) models are typically ineffective at capturing the high-dimensional and complex risk patterns that exist in this marginalized group. This paper suggests a hybrid predictive model that merges an ensemble feature selection strategy using ANOVA and mutual information and Harris Hawks optimization-tuned logistic regression and represents a new application of swarm intelligence to predict mental health in vulnerable groups. The explainable AI (XAI) methods can be used to understand the factors of trauma associated with model predictions. When applied to a group of 3,005 FSWs, it can be seen that the proposed model is more effective than traditional classifiers, with an accuracy of 95.78%, an F1 score of 95.77%, and an AUC of 0.96, and identifying post-traumatic stress, client-related violence, and occupational factors as major contributors to depression. This work bridges the gaps between conventional and ML approaches to develop an XAI tool that enables vulnerable groups to receive early assistance, evidence-based targeted psychosocial care, and health planning.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Trajectory Imitation: Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24064)
*Tianyuan Shi,Canbin Huang,Bei Li,Xin Chen,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SGPO提出策略引导策略优化框架，用可复用的策略蒸馏替代实例级轨迹模仿，通过前向KL散度与自适应实例加权，有效将强模型推理能力迁移至弱模型，在数学基准测试上超越SFT、同策略RL等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理能力蒸馏方法通常让弱模型模仿强模型的特定解题轨迹，这导致模型记忆实例特定步骤而非获取可迁移的问题解决技能，难以泛化到新问题上。

Method: SGPO包含三个关键组件：(1) 从强模型响应中提取结构化的策略描述；(2) 对每个问题同时构建自主轨迹和策略引导轨迹，直接对比有无策略引导的行为差异；(3) 采用token级前向KL散度选择性转移策略条件化引起的分布偏移，并通过自适应实例级权重在自主探索不足时加强引导、能力增长时减弱引导。

Result: 在四个数学基准测试、两个模型系列上的实验显示，SGPO一致优于SFT、同策略RL和混合策略基线，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上平均得分比最强基线高出2.2分。

Conclusion: 前向KL目标提供了内在选择性的蒸馏信号，优于直接轨迹模仿；策略蒸馏与基础模型能力展现出互补的扩展特性。

Abstract: Distilling reasoning capabilities from strong to weak language models typically involves imitating specific solution trajectories, effectively transferring what to answer rather than how to reason. This trajectory-level imitation encourages memorization of instance-specific steps rather than acquisition of transferable problem-solving skills, limiting generalization to novel problems. We propose Strategy-Guided Policy Optimization (SGPO), which replaces instance-level trajectory imitation with reusable strategy distillation. SGPO extracts structured strategy descriptions from strong-model responses and, for each problem, constructs both autonomous and strategy-guided trajectories to enable direct comparison of the model's behavior with and without strategic guidance. The framework then addresses two key questions. For how to distill, a token-level forward-KL objective selectively transfers the distributional shift induced by strategy conditioning into the unguided policy, with proximal constraints ensuring stability. For when to distill, adaptive instance-level weighting strengthens guidance when autonomous exploration falls short and reduces it as the model's own competence grows. Experiments on four mathematical benchmarks across two model families show that SGPO consistently outperforms SFT, on-policy RL, and hybrid-policy baselines, improving the average score by 2.2 points over the strongest baseline on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Analysis reveals that the forward-KL objective provides an inherently selective distillation signal that outperforms direct trajectory imitation, and that strategy distillation exhibits complementary scaling with base model capability.

</details>


### [94] [Exploring Academic Influence of Algorithms by Co-occurrence Network Based on Full-text of Academic Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24099)
*Yuzhuo Wang,Chengzhi Zhang,Min Song,Seong Deok Kim,Youngsoo Ko,Juhee Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于学术论文全文，构建了NLP领域的算法共现网络，从网络视角分析算法的群体影响力，发现算法网络具有复杂网络特征，经典高性能及处于不同研究时期交汇处的算法具有较高影响力，算法影响力下降时先失去核心网络位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常孤立地评估单个算法的影响力，忽视了算法之间通过互连形成的集体影响力。

Method: 利用深度学习模型提取算法实体，构建总体、累积和年度三种共现网络，采用多种中心性指标评估算法的群体影响力。

Result: 算法网络呈现复杂网络的典型特征，近二十年间连接日益密集；经典高性能算法及处于不同研究时期交汇处的算法具有高流行度、控制力和中心性；算法影响力下降时先失去核心网络位置。

Conclusion: 本研究首次大规模分析算法共现网络，提供了算法影响力的时序和结构视角，为未来算法、学者与任务间网络研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Algorithms have become central to scientific research in the era of artificial intelligence (AI). Although algorithm mentions in papers are often used to indicate popularity and influence, existing studies usually evaluate individual algorithms in isolation and pay limited attention to the collective influence formed through their interconnections. This study constructs large-scale algorithm co-occurrence networks in natural language processing (NLP) based on the full text of academic papers and investigates algorithm influence from a network perspective. Using deep learning models, we extract algorithm entities and build overall, cumulative, and annual co-occurrence networks. We analyze their structural characteristics and apply multiple centrality measures to assess the group influence of algorithms across the whole field and over time. The results show that algorithm networks display typical features of complex networks, with increasingly dense connections developing over approximately two decades. Classic, high-performing algorithms and those located at the intersections of different research periods tend to have high popularity, control, centrality, and balanced influence. When the influence of an algorithm declines, it usually loses its core network position first, followed by weaker associations with other algorithms. This study is the first large-scale analysis of algorithm co-occurrence networks. Covering more than four decades of academic publications, it provides a temporal and structural view of algorithm influence and offers a foundation for future research on networks linking algorithms, scholars, and tasks.

</details>


### [95] [ReMMD: Realistic Multilingual Multi-Image Agentic Verification for Multimodal Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24112)
*Chenhao Dang,Dantong Zhu,Jun Yang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMMD，一个面向真实世界多语言多图像场景的智能体式多模态虚假信息检测框架，包含包含500个样本、2756张图像、五语言、五类真实性标签的基准数据集ReMMDBench，以及基于持久记忆、将帖子分解为原子点并构建可复用证据集的智能体ReMMD-Agent。实验表明ReMMD-Agent在五类真实性分类中达到最优（GPT-5.2下准确率41.80%，宏F1 39.12%），同时相比MMD-Agent和T2-Agent分别降低17.5%和79.9%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态虚假信息检测基准和方法主要针对短文本、单图像、二元标签或单一操纵来源，与现实中包含长多语言叙述、多图像、混合来源及文本-图像帧错配的病毒式帖子不匹配；同时智能体验证在现实证据搜索下成本高昂，缺乏面向真实多语言多图像场景的统一验证框架。

Method: 提出ReMMD框架，包含两大部分：(1) ReMMDBench——真实世界基准，含500样本、2756张图像、五种单语言及两种跨语言设置、三种文本长度层级、多图像帖子、五类真实性标签、八类扭曲标签、证据来源及推理依据；(2) ReMMD-Agent——持久记忆验证智能体，将帖子分解为原子点，构建可复用的证据集，并预测结构化的L1/L2/L3输出。

Result: 在封闭系统、开源LVLM、MMD-Agent和T2-Agent的对比中，ReMMD-Agent在五类真实性分类上取得最佳性能（GPT-5.2下准确率41.80%，宏F1 39.12%），同时成本相比MMD-Agent降低17.5%，相比T2-Agent降低79.9%。

Conclusion: ReMMD框架有效填补了现有多模态虚假信息检测在真实多语言多图像场景下的空白，通过原子点分解和持久记忆证据集的设计，在提升检测性能的同时显著降低验证成本。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation detection is increasingly important because viral posts now combine long multilingual narratives, several images, mixed provenance, and subtle text--image framing errors. Existing benchmarks and methods remain poorly matched to this setting: they usually isolate short captions, single images, binary labels, or one manipulation source, while agentic verification remains costly under realistic evidence search. We present ReMMD, a realistic multilingual multi-image agentic verification framework for multimodal misinformation detection. ReMMD includes ReMMDBench, a real-world multimodal misinformation detection benchmark with 500 samples, 2,756 images, five monolingual languages, two cross-lingual settings, three text-length tiers, multi-image posts, five-way veracity labels, eight distortion labels, evidence provenance, and rationales. It also includes ReMMD-Agent, a persistent-memory verifier that decomposes posts into atomic points, builds a reusable evidence set, and predicts structured L1/L2/L3 outputs. Across proprietary systems, open LVLMs, MMD-Agent, and T2-Agent, ReMMD-Agent obtains the best five-way veracity performance, with 41.80% accuracy and 39.12% macro-F1 using GPT-5.2, while reducing cost by 17.5% relative to MMD-Agent and 79.9% relative to T2-Agent. The project is available at https://dang-ai.github.io/ReMMD.

</details>


### [96] [VeryTrace: Verifying Reasoning Traces through Compilable Formalism and Structured Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24124)
*Ninghan Zhong,Ahmet Ege Tanriverdi,Kaan Kale,Sriram Vishwanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeryTrace提出一种零样本验证与修复框架，通过领域特定语言（DSL）将自然语言推理轨迹形式化为可编译结构，结合确定性检查与LLM审计实现步骤级错误定位与修复，在数学、机器人规划与亲属推理任务上超越零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 思维链（CoT）提示的多步推理存在脆弱性：早期步骤的逻辑错误或幻觉会无声传播，产生自信但错误的结论。现有方法缺乏对推理轨迹的系统性验证与修复机制。

Method: 提出VeryTrace框架：1) 设计领域特定语言(DSL)，明确步骤依赖关系、将定量内容机械化可执行表达式、通过演绎模式结构化语义推理；2) 构建混合验证器，结合确定性检查（计算正确性、依赖解析、约束满足）与针对性LLM审计（针对不可机械化的语义判断），实现步骤级错误定位与修复。

Result: 在竞赛数学(AIME 2025)、机器人规划(LLM-BabyBench)和亲属推理(CLUTRR)三个不同领域，VeryTrace在无需领域特定训练或少样本示例的情况下，显著提升了最先进LLM的零样本推理准确率。

Conclusion: 形式化的轨迹验证能够在实现高精度的同时保持跨领域泛化能力，证明了结构化验证与修复范式在增强LLM推理可靠性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-step reasoning with Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting remains fragile: logical errors or hallucinations in early steps silently propagate, producing confident but incorrect conclusions. This paper presents VeryTrace, a zero-shot verification-and-repair framework that formalizes natural-language reasoning traces into a structured, compilable representation. VeryTrace introduces a Domain-Specific Language (DSL) that (i) makes step dependencies explicit, (ii) mechanizes quantitative content as executable expressions, and (iii) structures semantic inferences via deduction schemas. Our hybrid verifier combines deterministic checks for computational correctness, dependency resolution, and constraint satisfaction with targeted LLM audits for non-mechanizable semantic judgments, enabling step-level error localization and repair.
  Across three diverse domains-competition mathematics (AIME 2025), robotics planning (LLM-BabyBench), and kinship reasoning (CLUTRR), VeryTrace improves accuracy over zero-shot baselines on state-of-the-art LLMs without requiring domain-specific training or in-context examples, demonstrating that formalized trace verification achieves both precision and generalization.

</details>


### [97] [OmniPath: A Multi-Modal Agentic Framework for Auditing Wheelchair Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24129)
*ASM Mobarak Hossain,Nadim Mahmud,Vaskar Raychoudhury,Md Osman Gani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniPath是一个融合OSM路网拓扑与高密度航空LiDAR数据的系统，通过虚拟遍历和微尺度分析（0.5米增量），自动量化人行道表面的运行坡度、横向坡度和垂直不连续性等物理摩擦点，并依据ADA标准计算加权严重性评分，将障碍物分类为"轻微"到"严重"等级。系统在National Mall的200个地面实况调查中验证，对高严重性障碍物的F1分数达0.58-0.60，实现了从被动地图到主动环境审计的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准地图（如OSM）仅记录路径位置，未能传达轮椅使用者实际出行的物理体验，存在信息障碍。现有平台缺乏对微观物理摩擦点的自动量化能力，导致"隐形"障碍物未被标注。

Method: 融合OSM路网拓扑与USGS 3DEP高密度航空LiDAR数据，构建高保真3D模型；通过智能体虚拟遍历路网，以0.5米增量分析表面；量化运行坡度、横向坡度和垂直不连续性等参数，对照ADA标准计算加权严重性评分；采用分层随机抽样在National Mall进行200个地面实况验证。

Result: 对高严重性障碍物具有强诊断可靠性：Severe类F1=0.60，Critical类F1=0.58。

Conclusion: OmniPath通过自动化微观尺度检测，能够识别标准地图遗漏的"隐形"障碍物，将静态数据集转化为可预判无障碍挑战的数据源，实现用户出行前的主动环境审计。

Abstract: For a wheelchair user, a standard blue line on a map is often a broken promise. While platforms like OpenStreetMap (OSM) successfully capture where a path is, they frequently fail to convey how it physically feels to travel on it. This information barrier is problematic for wheelchair users. To solve this issue, we present OmniPath, a system that moves from passive mapping to proactive environmental auditing. Our framework fuses the network topology of OSM with the submeter precision of high-density aerial LiDAR (USGS 3DEP) to create a high-fidelity 3D model of the pedestrian environment. Rather than simply routing a user, our agent virtually traverses the network, analyzing the surface in 0.5 meter increments. It rigorously quantifies physical friction points specifically running slope, cross slope, and vertical discontinuities against ADA compliance standards, calculating a weighted severity score to categorize hazards from ``Mild'' to ``Critical.'' To ensure real world reliability, we validated the system against 200 physical ground truth field surveys across the National Mall using stratified random sampling. The framework demonstrated strong diagnostic reliability for high-severity hazards, achieving F1-scores of 0.60 for Severe and 0.58 for critical categories. By automating this micro-scale inspection, OmniPath identifies the ``invisible'' barriers that standard maps miss, effectively transforming a static dataset into accessibility data source that anticipates accessibility challenges before the user ever leaves home.

</details>


### [98] [T2D-Bench: Evidence-Gated Evaluation of LLM Outputs for Type 2 Diabetes Using a Multi-Layer Clinical-Lifestyle Knowledge Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24145)
*Saba A. Farahani,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: T2D-Bench是一个针对2型糖尿病的可复现基准测试和证据门控评估框架，用于检测LLM输出是否满足显式的、可在知识图谱中检查的证据要求。通过多层临床-生活方式知识图谱和约束性修订，该框架能在35%（GPT-4o-mini）和33%（GPT-4o）的案例中检测出无证据支持的遗漏，并使输出符合基准定义的证据标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在糖尿病管理上能生成临床流畅的建议，但常违反指南约束或无法明确证明与生活方式相关的血糖主张，缺乏可验证的证据追溯机制。

Method: 构建T2D-Bench：1）多层临床-生活方式知识图谱（UMLS、DrugBank、SIDER + ADA标准 + 生活方式-血糖机制桥接）；2）100个结构化临床场景（诊断、用药安全、对抗性生活方式冲突）；3）证据门控检测无证据支持的遗漏；4）约束性修订使输出达到验证器级别的合规性。

Result: GPT-4o-mini在35%的案例中未通过证据路径检查，GPT-4o为33%。证据门控能有效检测并纠正无支持的临床遗漏。

Conclusion: 可计算的证据约束能够使LLM输出中无支持的临床遗漏变得显式化、可测量和可纠正，为糖尿病领域的LLM安全性评估提供了结构化框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can produce clinically fluent recommendations for type 2 diabetes while failing to satisfy guideline constraints or explicitly justify lifestyle-related glycemic claims. We present T2D-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evidence-gated evaluation framework for testing whether LLM outputs satisfy explicit, graph-checkable evidence requirements. T2D-Bench is built on a multi-layer clinical-lifestyle knowledge graph that combines a biomedical spine (UMLS, DrugBank, SIDER), computable ADA Standards of Care rules, and lifestyle knowledge connected through a mechanistic bridge to glycemic laboratory effects. Across 100 structured vignettes spanning diagnosis, medication safety, and adversarial lifestyle conflicts, baseline outputs failed benchmark-defined evidence-path checks in 35% of cases for GPT-4o-mini and 33% for GPT-4o. The evidence gate detects unsupported omissions and uses constrained revision to bring outputs into verifier-level compliance with benchmark-defined evidence requirements. These results show that computable evidence constraints can make unsupported clinical omissions explicit, measurable, and correctable in diabetes-focused LLM outputs.

</details>


### [99] [The Geometry Behind Diffusion and Flow Matching: Gradient Flows and Geodesics in Wasserstein Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24157)
*Yian Yao,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，概率测度空间$\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d)$上的Wasserstein几何（W_2距离）统一了扩散模型与最优传输流匹配模型：扩散模型对应自由能$F(\rho)=\mathrm{KL}(\rho\|\pi)$的梯度流（Fokker-Planck方程），其JKO离散化即DDPM/DDIM/NCSN/SMLD/Energy Matching；流匹配模型对应同一流形上的Benamou-Brenier测地线（最优传输路径），生成过程为确定性ODE直路径。两类模型分别解决初值问题和边值问题，沿不同路径到达相同终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清扩散模型与最优传输流匹配模型之间的数学关系，指出它们实际上共享同一Wasserstein几何流形，而非独立的理论体系。

Method: 在$\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d)$的Wasserstein几何框架下，将扩散模型解释为自由能$F=\mathrm{KL}(\rho\|\pi)$的梯度流（Fokker-Planck方程），其隐式Euler离散化（JKO方案）对应DDPM/DDIM/NCSN/SMLD/Energy Matching；流匹配模型则对应同一流形上的Benamou-Brenier变分原理，其测地线即最优传输路径，生成过程为确定性ODE。

Result: 两类生成模型统一在同一Wasserstein几何框架下：扩散模型是自由能梯度流的初值问题，流匹配是测地线的边值问题，两者到达相同终点但路径不同。

Conclusion: Wasserstein几何为生成模型提供了统一的理论基础，揭示了扩散模型和流匹配模型之间的精确数学关系，有助于理解不同生成范式的本质联系。

Abstract: The space $\mathcal{P}_2(\mathbb{R}^d$) of probability measures with finite second moment carries a natural geometry: the quadratic Wasserstein distance W_2 makes it a complete metric space and, following Otto, a (formal) Riemannian manifold whose geodesics are the optimal-transport interpolations. On this manifold, the gradient flow of the free energy F(rho) = KL(rho || π) is exactly the Fokker-Planck equation, and its implicit-Euler discretization is the JKO scheme. This is the geometry underlying diffusion models: the forward process descends the free energy, and each denoising step realizes one JKO step, which recovers DDPM, DDIM, NCSN/SMLD, and Energy Matching; this is one scheme, not separate theories. The same manifold supports a second variational principle. Its geodesics - the minimum-action curves of the Benamou-Brenier formula - are precisely the optimal-transport paths that Flow Matching learns. Fixing both endpoints and following the geodesic, generation becomes a deterministic ODE along a straight line, hence far fewer sampling steps. Placing both families of models on one manifold makes their relationship exact: diffusion follows a free-energy gradient flow, an initial-value problem; optimal-transport Flow Matching follows a Wasserstein geodesic, a boundary-value problem. The two reach the same endpoints along different paths.

</details>


### [100] [An Introduction to Causal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24160)
*Elias Bareinboim,Junzhe Zhang,Sanghack Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了因果推断与强化学习这两个独立发展的领域之间的内在联系，指出它们都基于反事实关系这一共同基础。通过将强化学习环境建模为结构化因果模型，作者提出了因果强化学习（CRL）的统一框架，涵盖了在线学习、离策略学习和因果演算学习等多种范式，并引入了广义策略学习、模仿学习和反事实学习等新的学习模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断和强化学习虽然都依赖于反事实关系这一核心构建模块，但两个领域长期独立发展、缺乏交互。作者旨在揭示两者之间的内在联系，并通过数学化这一联系来创造新的学习机会。

Method: 将RL环境分解为具有不同因果不变性的自主机制集合，并采用结构化因果模型（SCM）进行形式化建模。基于此统一框架，将在线学习、离策略学习和因果演算学习等不同学习模式置于同一视角下处理。

Result: 提出了因果强化学习（CRL）这一新方向，引入了广义策略学习（含干预）、模仿学习和反事实学习等新颖的学习范式，拓展了传统强化学习的研究维度。

Conclusion: 因果推断与强化学习的交叉研究具有巨大潜力，CRL为理解和设计更广泛的反事实学习任务提供了统一的理论基础，值得两个领域共同深入探索。

Abstract: Causal inference provides a set of principles and tools that allow one to combine data and knowledge about an environment to reason with questions of counterfactual nature, i.e., what would have happened had reality been different, even when no data of this unrealized reality is currently available. Reinforcement learning provides methods to learn a policy that optimizes a specific measure (e.g., reward, regret) when the agent is deployed in an environment and pursues an exploratory, trial-and-error approach. These two disciplines have evolved independently and with virtually no interaction between them. We note that they operate over different aspects of the same building block, counterfactual relations, which makes them umbilically connected. Based on these observations, novel learning opportunities arise when this connection is explicitly acknowledged and mathematized. To realize this potential, we note that any environment where the RL agent is deployed can be decomposed as a collection of autonomous mechanisms with different causal invariances, parsimoniously modeled as a structural causal model; any standard RL setting implicitly encodes such a model. This formalization allows us to put under a unifying treatment different modes of learning, including online, off-policy, and causal calculus learning, which appear unrelated in the literature. However, these modalities are not exhaustive: we introduce several natural and pervasive classes of learning settings that entail novel dimensions of analysis. Specifically, we introduce and discuss through causal lenses generalized policy learning, where to intervene, imitation learning, and counterfactual learning. These tasks lead to a broader view of counterfactual learning and suggest great potential for studying causal inference and reinforcement learning side by side, which we call causal reinforcement learning (CRL).

</details>


### [101] [Data Scale, Not Latency, Shapes Cross-Lingual Encoder Transfer in Streaming ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24169)
*Nenad Banfic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了流式语音识别模型在迁移到新语言时，使用多语言（ML）编码器与仅英语（EN）编码器两种热启动方式的性能差异。通过对0.6B参数的FastConformer transducer在8种欧洲语言上的控制实验发现：多语言初始化的优势仅存在于低数据量场景，而非低延迟场景；随着目标语言数据量增加（100h→2500h），WER差距从4.21pp降至0.20pp，呈幂律衰减；流式延迟层级不影响该差距；4比特量化在匹配延迟下仅增加约0.5pp WER。结论建议：低数据用ML初始化，大数据时两者无差异，延迟和量化决策可独立进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决流式语音识别模型迁移到新语言时，多语言编码器与仅英语编码器两种热启动方式的优劣比较问题，澄清"多语言初始化在低数据时更有优势"这一直觉性认知的持续时间、是否受流式延迟影响、以及是否能在量化部署下保持。

Method: 采用控制变量法，在0.6B参数的cache-aware FastConformer transducer上，对8种欧洲语言进行系统实验。自变量包括：目标语言数据规模（100h~2500h共5个等级）、流式延迟层级（3个流式层级+离线解码）、测试集（最多4个公开测试集）。通过比较EN与ML初始化的WER差距及其随各因素的变化规律进行分析，并用幂律拟合数据量-优势衰减关系。

Result: 1) 多语言初始化是数据受限优势而非延迟受限优势：FLEURS上160ms延迟时，EN-ML WER差距从100h的+4.21pp降至2500h的+0.20pp，呈幂律衰减（目标语言数据每翻倍，剩余优势约减半）。2) 3个流式延迟层级下，100~1000h范围内EN-ML差距大致稳定，2500h时接近零。3) 4比特权重量化在560ms匹配延迟下，编码器占用减少约3倍，FLEURS WER仅增加约0.5pp。

Conclusion: 提供简单实用的指导原则：低数据量场景使用多语言初始化，大数据量时两种初始化方式效果基本等价，延迟和量化决策可以独立于初始化选择进行。

Abstract: Adapting a streaming speech recognition model to a new language requires choosing between two plausible warm starts: a multilingual (ML) encoder or an English-only (EN) encoder. The common intuition is that the multilingual encoder should help most at low data, but it is unclear how long that advantage persists, whether tight streaming latency amplifies it, and whether it survives deployment quantization. We answer these questions with a controlled sweep of a 0.6 B-parameter cache-aware FastConformer transducer across eight European languages, up to five target-language data scales (100 h to 2500 h), three streaming tiers plus offline decoding, and up to four public test sets. The main result is that multilingual initialization is a data-limited advantage, not a latency-limited one. On FLEURS at 160 ms, the mean EN-ML word error rate (WER) gap falls from +4.21 percentage points (pp) at 100 h to +0.20 pp at 2500 h; a power-law fit summarizes this decay, with each doubling of target-language data roughly halving the remaining advantage. Across the three streaming tiers, the across-language mean EN-ML gap is approximately stable at each scale from 100 to 1000 h, and is near zero by 2500 h. Finally, 4-bit weight-only encoder quantization at the matched 560 ms streaming tier reduces the encoder footprint by about 3x, with an average FLEURS WER increase of about 0.5 pp. The resulting guideline is simple: use multilingual initialization in low-data regimes, treat the choice as effectively irrelevant at large data, and make latency and quantization decisions independently.

</details>


### [102] [Navigating User Behavior toward Personalized Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24196)
*Hengji Zhou,Yufeng Liu,Ye Liu,Yong Xu,Lianghao Xia,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NaviGen通过双重标识符（协同编码+文本编码）将用户行为历史转化为可执行的生成指令，采用两阶段SFT+RL训练流程，在多个领域显著提升个性化图像/视频生成质量和指令准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGC管线假设用户能提供完善的创作指令，但终端用户很少能清晰阐述视觉细节，导致生成结果与用户需求错位。需要一种方法将用户交互历史转化为下游合成模型可执行的指令。

Method: 提出NaviGen框架：1) 为每个物品设计双重标识符（协同编码+文本编码），作为行为基底和语义桥梁；2) 两阶段SFT+RL流程：先通过进化搜索监督蒸馏偏好推理和指令写作能力，再通过分层自洽奖励对齐生成与用户意图。

Result: 在商品、游戏和短视频三个领域，NaviGen提升了个性化图像和视频生成质量，增强了下一物品预测能力，生成了更具体、相关且可视觉生成的指令。

Conclusion: NaviGen有效解决了行为编码的语言可读性和指令写作技能缺失两大障碍，为个性化内容生成提供了一种统一解决方案。

Abstract: Modern AIGC pipelines deliver high-fidelity images and videos but presuppose a well-formed creation instruction, while end users rarely articulate visual details, leaving generators misaligned with user demand. We study personalized content generation, which turns a user's interaction history into an executable instruction for downstream synthesis, and identify two obstacles: behavior must be encoded in a form legible to language reasoning, and the model must acquire instruction-writing skill absent from both pretraining and behavior data. We propose NaviGen, which represents each item with a dual identifier coupling a collaborative code and a textual code as a behavioral substrate and a semantic bridge in one token stream. On this representation, a two-stage SFT+RL pipeline first distills preference reasoning and instruction writing from evolutionarily searched supervision, then aligns generation with user intent through hierarchical and self-consistent rewards. Experiments across product, game, and short-video domains show that NaviGen improves personalized image and video generation, strengthens next-item prediction, and yields more specific, relevant, and visually generatable instructions. Our code is anonymously released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/NaviGen.

</details>


### [103] [Exploring the relationship between human-centric AI and firm idiosyncratic risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24224)
*Zhen-Yuan Ralph Liu,Yu-Ting Wang,Jia-Jia Yan,Shivam Gupta,Mihalis Giannakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了工业5.0背景下以人为本的人工智能（HCAI）对企业特质风险（IR）的影响，基于2015-2023年中国上市企业面板数据发现HCAI能够降低企业IR，且数字化和高管持股强化了这一效应，而运营效率和具有IT背景的CEO则弱化该效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管工业5.0中关于HCAI的讨论广泛，但其对企业特质风险的影响尚未得到充分探索。特质风险反映了投资者对企业差异化AI战略和实施的反应，在当前技术革命背景下，理解HCAI如何影响企业财务风险是一个重要且紧迫的问题。

Method: 整合情境化AI理论与社会技术系统理论，将HCAI概念化为一种情境化AI策略。使用2015至2023年中国上市企业的多源面板数据集进行实证分析。

Result: HCAI与企业更低的特质风险相关。数字化和高管持股加强了这一风险降低效应，而运营效率和具有IT背景的CEO则意外地削弱了该效应。

Conclusion: 研究为AI时代的伦理AI治理和企业财务风险管理提供了理论贡献和实践启示。

Abstract: Despite the extensive discussions of human-centric AI (HCAI) in Industry 5.0, its effects on firms' idiosyncratic risks (IR) remains underexplored. This is an imperative issue for firms navigate financial risks during the current technological revolution, as IR reflects investor reactions to corporate heterogeneous AI strategies and implementations by isolating firm-level stock volatility from systematic factors. Integrating situated AI theory with social-technical systems theory, we conceptualise HCAI as a situated AI strategy that reduces AI-related ethical risks and fosters AI-Human synergies in firms' business operations, ultimately reducing IR by aligning with stakeholders' diverse expectations. Moreover, socio-technical factors, namely digitalisation, operational efficiency, executive shareholding, and CEOs with IT background, may moderate the HCAI-IR relationship. Using a multi-source panel dataset of Chinese listed firms from 2015 to 2023, we find that HCAI is associated with lower firm IR. Furthermore, digitalisation and executive shareholding strengthen this risk-reducing effect, whereas operational efficiency and CEOs with IT background surprisingly attenuate it. Our findings offer theoretical contributions and practical insights for both ethical AI governance and firm financial risk management in the AI era.

</details>


### [104] [FlowR2A: Learning Reward-to-Action Distribution for Multimodal Driving Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24231)
*Xirui Li,Zhe Liu,Xiaoqing Ye,Wenhua Han,Yifeng Pan,Junyu Han,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowR2A通过将仿真奖励从判别目标重构为生成条件，利用流匹配解码器学习奖励条件化的动作分布，统一了基于评分方法的密集监督和基于锚点方法的提案生成，在NAVSIM基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多模态驾驶规划中两类范式（基于评分的方法与基于锚点的方法）之间的长期矛盾：前者受密集奖励监督但限于固定动作词汇，后者能动态生成提案但受限于单一真值轨迹的稀疏监督。

Method: 1) 将仿真奖励从判别目标重构为生成条件；2) 利用流匹配解码器从密集轨迹-奖励对中学习奖励条件化的动作分布；3) 引入细粒度逐时间步奖励条件化与奖励噪声增强；4) 通过奖励引导和锚定采样实现可控测试时采样。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准上达到最先进结果，生成的多模态提案质量显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: FlowR2A通过生成式框架统一了密集监督与动态提案生成，使模型内化动作在安全性、进度、舒适性和规则合规性方面的结果关联，同时通过奖励条件化和噪声增强平衡硬安全约束与软进度目标。

Abstract: Multimodal driving planning faces a long-standing tension between two paradigms: scoring-based methods benefit from dense reward supervision but are confined to a fixed action vocabulary, while anchor-based methods generate proposals dynamically yet suffer from sparse supervision constrained to a single ground-truth trajectory. In this work, we propose FlowR2A, which resolves this tension by reframing simulation-based rewards from discriminative targets into generative conditions. By learning the reward-conditioned action distribution from dense trajectory-reward pairs with a flow-matching decoder, FlowR2A unifies the dense supervision of scoring-based methods with the proposal generation of anchor-based methods in a single generative model, forcing the model to internalize the correlation between an action and its outcomes in safety, progress, comfort, and rule compliance. To balance hard safety constraints against soft progress objectives, we introduce fine-grained per-timestep reward conditioning and reward noise augmentation. The generative formulation naturally supports controllable test-time sampling via reward guidance and anchored sampling, producing high-quality proposals. FlowR2A achieves state-of-the-art results on the NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks, with multimodal proposals of substantially higher quality than prior methods.

</details>


### [105] [SP-Mind: An Autonomous Reasoning Agent for Spatial Proteomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24235)
*Yucheng Yuan,Yuanfeng Ji,Zhongxiao Li,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SP-Mind 是首个自主AI智能体，统一了空间蛋白质组学分析流程，从原始多重复用组织成像到下游表型发现，在SP-Bench基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间蛋白质组学分析工作流碎片化严重，需要专家手动编排异构工具，限制了研究的可扩展性和可重复性。

Method: 提出SP-Mind自主AI智能体，配备专家策划的生物分析技能和专用计算工具，将自然语言查询转化为端到端分析工作流，无需任务特定微调。同时构建SP-Bench基准，涵盖18个类别共102项任务。

Result: 在SP-Bench和既定下游任务上的广泛评估表明，SP-Mind相比现有开源生物医学智能体基线取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: SP-Mind能够统一空间蛋白质组学分析流程，提升研究的可扩展性和可重复性。

Abstract: Spatial proteomics enables single-cell-resolution characterization of protein expression within tissue architecture, playing a critical role in understanding tumor microenvironments and guiding precision medicine. However, current analysis workflows remain fragmented, requiring expert manual orchestration of heterogeneous tools and limiting research scalability and reproducibility. We present SP-Mind, the first autonomous AI agent designed to unify the spatial proteomics analysis pipeline, from raw multiplexed tissue imaging to downstream phenotype discovery. Equipped with expert-curated biological analysis skills and specialized computational tools, SP-Mind converts natural-language queries into end-to-end analytical workflows without task-specific fine-tuning. To rigorously evaluate its capabilities, we introduce SP-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning diverse tissue types, comprising 102 tasks across 18 distinct categories. Through extensive evaluation on SP-Bench and established downstream tasks, SP-Mind achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to existing open-source biomedical agent baselines.

</details>


### [106] [Towards Federated Long-Tailed Graph Learning: An Energy-Guided Dual Decoupling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24237)
*Lianshuai Guo,Zhongzheng Yuan,Xunkai Li,Meixia Qu,Wenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedEPD框架，通过拓扑纯化与语义重校准的双解耦范式，解决联邦图学习中数据长尾分布导致的模型偏差和少数类节点表征退化问题，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图学习方法在处理长尾分布数据时存在两个关键问题：一是全局模型偏向多数类；二是少数类节点被异质性主导的头部类邻域结构隔离。传统的拓扑无关统计补偿方法在数据稀缺时不仅无法恢复尾部节点，反而因过拟合邻域结构噪声导致表征退化。

Method: FedEPD采用双解耦范式：(1) 拓扑纯化：基于分布感知的Dirichlet能量剪枝过滤空间异质性边；(2) 语义重校准：从拓扑中心节点提取鲁棒的全局原型，通过空间低通原型注入整合到局部表征中；此外采用两阶段交替优化策略保护多数类决策边界同时提升少数类准确率。

Result: 在多个长尾基准数据集上，FedEPD实现了SOTA性能，Accuracy绝对提升达4.97%，Macro-F1绝对提升达5.48%。

Conclusion: FedEPD通过拓扑与语义的解耦处理，有效缓解了联邦图学习中长尾分布带来的性能退化，验证了双重解耦范式在该场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Federated Graph Learning facilitates collaborative graph modeling across distributed clients while preserving data privacy. However, real-world data categories frequently exhibit long-tailed distributions. Such statistical scarcity severely degrades performance in two ways: it biases the global model toward majority classes, and it structurally isolates minority nodes by submerging them in heterophilic, head-dominated neighborhoods. While existing methods attempt topology-agnostic statistical compensations, they often fail under data scarcity. Instead of recovering tail nodes, they overfit the structural noise from adjacent dominant classes, leading to representation degradation. To address these limitations, we propose FedEPD, a framework built on a dual decoupling paradigm that separates topological purification from semantic recalibration. Specifically, FedEPD utilizes distribution-aware Dirichlet energy pruning to filter spatial heterophilic edges. It then overcomes Non-IID distribution shifts by extracting robust global prototypes from topologically central nodes, which are incorporated into local representations via a spatial low-pass prototype injection. Furthermore, a two stage alternating optimization strategy strictly protects majority decision boundaries while improving minority accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedEPD achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse long-tailed benchmarks, yielding absolute improvements of up to 4.97% in Accuracy and 5.48% in Macro-F1.

</details>


### [107] [Probing the Misaligned Thinking Process of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24251)
*Kaiwen Zhou,Constantin Venhoff,Jonathan Michala,Xin Eric Wang,William Saunders*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将大模型的失校对行为分解为细粒度认知过程（失校对指标），并使用线性探针从模型内部激活中检测这些指标。构建了18个指标的分类体系、自动化多轮对话生成流程，在5种失校对行为上达到0.935 AUROC，同时保持较低的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在关键场景中的部署日益增多，但其表现出策略性欺骗、sandbagging、自我保存等失校对行为，需要可靠检测方法来确保安全使用。

Method: 1) 将失校对分解为18个细粒度认知指标（如欺骗性推理、自我保存意识等）；2) 设计元计划引导的自动化流水线生成多轮训练对话；3) 使用线性探针从模型内部激活检测这些指标；4) 构建包含自动化行为诱导、基准测试和自然对话的OOD评估套件。

Result: 在5种失校对行为上，线性探针在OOD基准测试中达到0.935 AUROC，等价于强LLM判别器，且在良性对话上保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 细粒度认知指标分解结合内部激活探针可有效检测失校对行为，具备良好的泛化能力，为模型安全监控提供了一种可解释且可靠的方法。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit a growing range of misaligned behaviors such as strategic deception, sandbagging, and self-preservation. As they are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings, it is critical to reliably detect such behaviors to ensure safe and responsible use. In this work, we propose to monitor misalignment by decomposing it into fine-grained cognitive processes -- misalignment indicators -- and detecting their presence in a model's internal activations via linear probes. We develop a taxonomy of 18 indicators spanning different misaligned behaviors, paired with an automated, meta-plan-guided pipeline that generates multi-turn training conversations. To rigorously evaluate generalization, we construct an out-of-distribution suite combining automated behavioral elicitation, established misalignment benchmarks, and natural benign conversations. Across 5 misaligned behaviors, our probes match a strong LLM judge with 0.935 AUROC on out-of-distribution benchmarks while keeping a low false positive rate on benign traffic. We further perform in-depth analysis to understand the probes and the model's internal representations of misalignment indicators.

</details>


### [108] [Tractable Reasoning and Conjunctive Query Answering for Defeasible DL-Lite under Rational Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24279)
*Giovanni Casini,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了理性闭包（Rational Closure, RC）在DL-Lite家族轻量级描述逻辑的核心与Horn变体上的应用，提出了一种基于现有经典推理器的插件架构，实现了RC下的实例检测和合取查询回答的高效计算，且计算开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在描述逻辑中，理性闭包是一种处理可废止知识的非单调推理形式，已被广泛认可。然而，RC在DL-Lite这类轻量级描述逻辑上的应用尚未充分研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一种插件架构，构建在现有标准经典推理器之上，用于支持DL-Lite在RC下的实例检测和合取查询回答。

Result: 证明了在RC下，DL-Lite的推理和CQ回答可以高效完成，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 所提出的插件架构能够以最小计算开销实现DL-Lite在理性闭包下的非单调推理，具有实用价值。

Abstract: In Description Logics (DLs), reasoning under Rational Closure (RC) is a well-known and widely accepted non-monotonic formalism to handle defeasible knowledge. In this paper, we study the application of RC to the core and horn variants of the DL-Lite family of lightweight description logics. We analyze both entitlement (instance checking) and Conjunctive Query (CQ) answering under RC. Our main contribution is providing a plug-in architecture that builds upon existing standard classical reasoners, establishing that reasoning and CQ answering under RC for DL-Lite can be done efficiently with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [109] [LemonHarness Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24311)
*Kailong Ren,Fubo Sun,Jiachen Liu,Liu Yang,Zimo Yin,Jiaying Li,Congli Yin,Ming He,Yu Huo,Jiawei Liu,Zeping Chen,Yubin Huangfu,Ronghua Li,Yixuan Wu,Xing Su,Yanzhi Xu,Likang Wu,Hongke Zhao,Lei Zhang,Xiaohui Geng,Jianping Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LemonHarness是一个面向长周期LLM Agent的集成执行框架，通过明确的执行边界、结构化工具接口、可复用规则知识库和时间感知执行机制，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上取得了84.49%-86.52%的准确率，有效提升了长任务执行的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在执行长周期任务时，由于缺少明确的工作空间边界，状态变更操作（如文件写入、临时工件生成）会散落在文件系统中，导致修改状态难以追踪，累积的弱约束变更降低了执行稳定性。

Method: 1) 建立显式执行边界，将模型调用、工具执行和规则知识纳入统一受控边界内；2) 通过结构化工具接口执行状态变更操作，执行反馈作为观测供后续模型决策使用；3) 引入可复用规则知识库，将执行规则和验收标准转化为运行时知识；4) 添加时间感知执行机制，向模型暴露已用和剩余时间预算，使其能动态平衡探索、实现和验证的投入。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，LemonHarness_GPT-5.3-CodeX在445次试验中达到84.49%准确率；使用更强的GPT-5.5骨干网络后，平均准确率提升至86.52%。

Conclusion: 统一的运行时边界、可调用的规则知识以及时间感知执行能够显著提升长周期Agent的执行稳定性。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents are applied to longer tasks, they increasingly modify workspace state across multiple rounds of iteration. However, agents typically observe only tool outputs and log fragments, while the actual state changes occur in the file system. Without explicit workspace boundaries, state-changing operations such as file writes and temporary artifact generation may scatter changes across paths. Over time, these weakly constrained changes accumulate, making states such as modified files difficult to track. This paper presents LemonHarness, an integrated execution framework for long-horizon agents. LemonHarness establishes an explicit execution boundary by constraining state-changing operations within a clearly defined workspace and bringing model invocation, tool execution, and rule knowledge within a single controlled boundary. State-changing operations, including file writes, dependency installation, and temporary artifact creation, are executed through structured tool interfaces, with execution feedback recorded as observations available to subsequent model decisions. The system also introduces a reusable rule knowledge base, which turns recurring execution rules and acceptance criteria into runtime knowledge. LemonHarness further adds a time-aware execution mechanism that exposes elapsed and remaining budget to the model, so it can rebalance exploration, implementation, and validation effort as time pressure shifts and avoid timeouts from long waits or excessive verification. On Terminal-Bench 2.0, LemonHarness_GPT-5.3-CodeX reached 84.49% accuracy over 445 trials; pairing the same framework with the stronger GPT-5.5 backbone raised the average accuracy to 86.52% across five jobs. The results suggest that a unified runtime boundary, callable rule knowledge, and time-aware execution can improve the stability of long-horizon agent execution.

</details>


### [110] [Prob-BBDM: a Probabilistic Brownian Bridge Diffusion Model for MRI sequence image-to-image translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24313)
*Martin Valls,Pascal Bourdon,Christine Fernandez-Maloigne,Guillaume Herpe,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于布朗桥扩散模型（Prob-BBDM）的MRI序列合成方法，仅需4步扩散即可从2D轴向切片高质量生成MRI，在BraTS 2021上SSIM达88.46%、PSNR达26.09 dB，合成图像用于肿瘤分割Dice达88.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态医学图像分析对优化检查质量至关重要，但获取多种3D成像模态在临床中资源消耗大且耗时，亟需高效、高质量的图像翻译方法。

Method: 提出Probabilistic-BBDM模型，将变分编码器引导的扩散机制与布朗桥扩散模型结合，利用概率图像分布增强合成质量，仅需4步扩散步骤实现高效推理。

Result: 在BraTS 2021数据集上，SSIM达88.46%、PSNR达26.09 dB；仅需4步扩散；在外部数据集上保持一致性；合成切片用于肿瘤分割Dice为88.71%、HD95为3.49 mm。

Conclusion: Prob-BBDM实现了高质量、高效率且泛化性强的MRI序列合成，合成图像保留了关键诊断信息，为医学图像翻译提供了一种有前景的方案。
